```text
  583  void ImageDownloadHandle::update()
  584  {
  585  	if (mOverQuotaPendingTime > 0)
  586  	{
  587  		int lastTime = SDL_GetTicks();
  588  		if (lastTime - mOverQuotaPendingTime > mOverQuotaRetryDelay)
  589  		{
  590  			mOverQuotaPendingTime = 0;
  591  
  592  			LOG(LogDebug) << "REQ_429_TOOMANYREQUESTS : Retrying";
  593  
  594  			std::string url = mRequest->getUrl();
  595  			delete mRequest;
  596  			mRequest = new HttpReq(url, mSavePath);
  597  		}
  598  
  599  		return;
  600  	}
  601  
  602  	HttpReq::Status status = mRequest->status();
  603  
  604  	if (status == HttpReq::REQ_IN_PROGRESS)
  605  		return;
  606  	
  607  	if (status == HttpReq::REQ_429_TOOMANYREQUESTS)
  608  	{
  609  		mRetryCount++;
  610  		if (mRetryCount >= mOverQuotaRetryCount)
  611  		{
  612  			setStatus(ASYNC_DONE); // Ignore error
  613  			return;
  614  		}
  615  
  616  		setStatus(ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS);
  617  
  618  		auto retryDelay = mRequest->getResponseHeader("Retry-After");
  619  		if (!retryDelay.empty())
  620  		{
  621  			mOverQuotaRetryCount = 1;
  622  			mOverQuotaRetryDelay = Utils::String::toInteger(retryDelay) * 1000;
  623  		}
  624  
  625  		mOverQuotaPendingTime = SDL_GetTicks();
  626  		LOG(LogDebug) << "REQ_429_TOOMANYREQUESTS : Retrying in " << mOverQuotaRetryDelay << " seconds";
  627  		return;
  628  	}
  629  
  630  	// Ignored errors
  631  	if (status == HttpReq::REQ_404_NOTFOUND || status == HttpReq::REQ_IO_ERROR)
  632  	{
  633  		setStatus(ASYNC_DONE);
  634  		return;
  635  	}
  636  
  637  	// Blocking errors
  638  	if (status != HttpReq::REQ_SUCCESS)
  639  	{
  640  		setError(status, Utils::String::removeHtmlTags(mRequest->getErrorMsg()));
  641  		return;
  642  	}
  643  
  644  	if (status == HttpReq::REQ_SUCCESS && mStatus == ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS)
  645  	{
  646  		std::string ext = Utils::String::toLower(Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(mSavePath));
  647  
  648  		// Make sure extension is the good one, according to the response 'Content-Type'
  649  		std::string contentType = mRequest->getResponseHeader("Content-Type");
  650  		if (!contentType.empty())
  651  		{
  652  			std::string trueExtension;
  653  			if (Utils::String::startsWith(contentType, "image/"))
  654  			{
  655  				trueExtension = "." + contentType.substr(6);
  656  				if (trueExtension == ".jpeg")
  657  					trueExtension = ".jpg";
  658  				else if (trueExtension == ".svg+xml")
  659  					trueExtension = ".svg";
  660  
  661  			}
  662  			else if (Utils::String::startsWith(contentType, "video/"))
  663  			{
  664  				trueExtension = "." + contentType.substr(6);
  665  				if (trueExtension == ".quicktime")
  666  					trueExtension = ".mov";
  667  			}
  668  
  669  			if (!trueExtension.empty() && trueExtension != ext)
  670  			{
  671  				auto newFileName = Utils::FileSystem::changeExtension(mSavePath, trueExtension);
  672  				if (Utils::FileSystem::renameFile(mSavePath, newFileName))
  673  				{
  674  					mSavePath = newFileName;
  675  					ext = trueExtension;
  676  				}
  677  			}
  678  		}
  679  
  680  		// It's an image ?
  681  		if (mSavePath.find("-fanart") == std::string::npos && mSavePath.find("-bezel") == std::string::npos && mSavePath.find("-map") == std::string::npos && (ext == ".jpg" || ext == ".jpeg" || ext == ".png" || ext == ".bmp" || ext == ".gif"))
  682  		{
  683  			try { resizeImage(mSavePath, mMaxWidth, mMaxHeight); }
  684  			catch(...) { }
  685  		}
  686  	}
  687  
  688  	setStatus(ASYNC_DONE);
  689  }
  690  
  691  //you can pass 0 for width or height to keep aspect ratio
  692  bool resizeImage(const std::string& path, int maxWidth, int maxHeight)
  693  {
  694  	// nothing to do
  695  	if(maxWidth == 0 && maxHeight == 0)
  696  		return true;
  697  
  698  	FREE_IMAGE_FORMAT format = FIF_UNKNOWN;
  699  	FIBITMAP* image = NULL;
  700  	
  701  	//detect the filetype
  702  #if WIN32
  703  	format = FreeImage_GetFileTypeU(Utils::String::convertToWideString(path).c_str(), 0);
  704  	if(format == FIF_UNKNOWN)
  705  		format = FreeImage_GetFIFFromFilenameU(Utils::String::convertToWideString(path).c_str());
  706  #else
  707  	format = FreeImage_GetFileType(path.c_str(), 0);
  708  	if(format == FIF_UNKNOWN)
  709  		format = FreeImage_GetFIFFromFilename(path.c_str());
  710  #endif
  711  	if(format == FIF_UNKNOWN)
  712  	{
  713  		LOG(LogError) << "Error - could not detect filetype for image \"" << path << "\"!";
  714  		return false;
  715  	}
  716  
  717  	//make sure we can read this filetype first, then load it
  718  	if(FreeImage_FIFSupportsReading(format))
  719  	{
  720  #if WIN32
  721  		image = FreeImage_LoadU(format, Utils::String::convertToWideString(path).c_str());
  722  #else
  723  		image = FreeImage_Load(format, path.c_str());
  724  #endif
  725  	}else{
  726  		LOG(LogError) << "Error - file format reading not supported for image \"" << path << "\"!";
  727  		return false;
  728  	}
  729  
  730  	float width = (float)FreeImage_GetWidth(image);
  731  	float height = (float)FreeImage_GetHeight(image);
  732  
  733  	if (width == 0 || height == 0)
  734  	{
  735  		FreeImage_Unload(image);
  736  		return true;
  737  	}
  738  
  739  	if(maxWidth == 0)
  740  		maxWidth = (int)((maxHeight / height) * width);
  741  	else if(maxHeight == 0)
  742  		maxHeight = (int)((maxWidth / width) * height);
  743  	
  744  	if (width <= maxWidth && height <= maxHeight)
  745  	{
  746  		FreeImage_Unload(image);
  747  		return true;
  748  	}
  749  		
  750  	FIBITMAP* imageRescaled = FreeImage_Rescale(image, maxWidth, maxHeight, FILTER_BILINEAR);
  751  	FreeImage_Unload(image);
  752  
  753  	if(imageRescaled == NULL)
  754  	{
  755  		LOG(LogError) << "Could not resize image! (not enough memory? invalid bitdepth?)";
  756  		return false;
  757  	}
  758  
  759  	bool saved = false;
  760  	
  761  	try
  762  	{
  763  #if WIN32
  764  		saved = (FreeImage_SaveU(format, imageRescaled, Utils::String::convertToWideString(path).c_str()) != 0);
  765  #else
  766  		saved = (FreeImage_Save(format, imageRescaled, path.c_str()) != 0);
  767  #endif
  768  	}
  769  	catch(...) { }
  770  
  771  	FreeImage_Unload(imageRescaled);
  772  
  773  	if(!saved)
  774  		LOG(LogError) << "Failed to save resized image!";
  775  
  776  	return saved;
  777  }
  778  
  779  std::string Scraper::getSaveAsPath(FileData* game, const MetaDataId metadataId, const std::string& extension)
  780  {
  781  	std::string suffix = "image";
  782  	std::string folder = "images";
  783  
  784  	switch (metadataId)
  785  	{
  786  	case MetaDataId::Thumbnail: suffix = "thumb"; break;
  787  	case MetaDataId::Marquee: suffix = "marquee"; break;
  788  	case MetaDataId::Video: suffix = "video"; folder = "videos"; break;
  789  	case MetaDataId::FanArt: suffix = "fanart"; break;
  790  	case MetaDataId::BoxBack: suffix = "boxback"; break;
  791  	case MetaDataId::BoxArt: suffix = "box"; break;
  792  	case MetaDataId::Wheel: suffix = "wheel"; break;
  793  	case MetaDataId::TitleShot: suffix = "titleshot"; break;
  794  	case MetaDataId::Manual: suffix = "manual"; folder = "manuals";  break;
  795  	case MetaDataId::Magazine: suffix = "magazine"; folder = "magazines"; break;
  796  	case MetaDataId::Map: suffix = "map"; break;
  797  	case MetaDataId::Cartridge: suffix = "cartridge"; break;
  798  	case MetaDataId::Bezel: suffix = "bezel"; break;
  799  	}
  800  
  801  	auto system = game->getSourceFileData()->getSystem();
  802  
  803  	const std::string subdirectory = system->getName();
  804  	const std::string name = Utils::FileSystem::getStem(game->getPath()) + "-" + suffix;
  805  
  806  	std::string path = system->getRootFolder()->getPath() + "/" + folder + "/";
  807  
  808  	if(!Utils::FileSystem::exists(path))
  809  		Utils::FileSystem::createDirectory(path);
  810  
  811  	path += name + extension;
  812  	return path;
  813  }
```

```text
   19  	if (p.running && !p.finished)

   24  ThreadedScraper::ThreadedScraper(Window* window, const std::queue<ScraperSearchParams>& searches, int threadCount)
   25  	: mSearchQueue(searches), mWindow(window)
   26  {
   27  	mExitCode = ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS;
   28  	mTotal = (int) mSearchQueue.size();

   30  }
   31  
   32  void ThreadedScraper::Process()
   33  {
   34  	{
   35  		// A fresh run: the page reads this from its first frame.
   36  		std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sProgressMutex);
   37  		sProgress = Progress();
   38  		sProgress.running = true;
   39  		sProgress.total = mTotal;
   40  		sStarted = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
   41  	}
   42  
   43  	for (int i = 0; i < mThreadCount; i++)
   44  	{
   45  		if (mSearchQueue.size() == 0)
   46  			break;
   47  
   48  		ScraperThread* thread = new ScraperThread(i);
   49  		mScraperThreads.push_back(thread);
   50  		ProcessNextGame(thread);
   51  	}
   52  
   53  	mHandle = new std::thread(&ThreadedScraper::run, this);
   54  }

   56  void ThreadedScraper::ProcessNextGame(ScraperThread* thread)
   57  {

   59  	mSearchQueue.pop();
   60  	mCurrentGame = item.getGameName();
   61  
   62  	LOG(LogInfo) << "[Thread " << thread->mThreadId << "] ProcessNextGame : " << mCurrentGame;

   66  	updateUI();

   91  void ScraperThread::run(const ScraperSearchParams& params)
   92  {
   93  	mResult = ScraperSearchResult();
   94  	mErrorStatus = 0;
   95  	mStatusString = "";
   96  	mStatus = ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS;
   97  	mSearch = params;
   98  	mMDResolveHandle.reset();
   99  
  100  	mSearchHandle = Scraper::getScraper()->search(params);

  102  
  103  int ScraperThread::updateState()
  104  {
  105  	if (mSearchHandle && mSearchHandle->status() != ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS)
  106  	{
  107  		auto status = mSearchHandle->status();
  108  		auto results = mSearchHandle->getResults();
  109  		auto statusString = mSearchHandle->getStatusString();
  110  		auto httpCode = mSearchHandle->getErrorCode();
  111  
  112  		LOG(LogInfo) << "[Thread " << mThreadId << "] ThreadedScraper::SearchResponse : " << httpCode << " " << statusString;
  113  
  114  		mSearchHandle.reset();
  115  
  116  		if (status == ASYNC_DONE)
  117  		{
  118  			if (results.size() > 0)
  119  			{
  120  				if (results[0].hasMedia())
  121  					mMDResolveHandle = results[0].resolveMetaDataAssets(mSearch);
  122  				else
  123  					acceptResult(results[0]);
  124  			}
  125  			else
  126  			{
  127  				mStatus = ASYNC_DONE;
  128  				mErrorStatus = 0;
  129  			}
  130  		}
  131  		else if (status == ASYNC_ERROR)
  132  			processError(httpCode, statusString);
  133  	}
  134  
  135  	if (mMDResolveHandle && mMDResolveHandle->status() != ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS)
  136  	{
  137  		auto status = mMDResolveHandle->status();
  138  		auto result = mMDResolveHandle->getResult();
  139  		auto statusString = mMDResolveHandle->getStatusString();
  140  		auto httpCode = mMDResolveHandle->getErrorCode();
  141  
  142  		LOG(LogInfo) << "[Thread " << mThreadId << "] ResolveResponse : " << statusString;
  143  
  144  		mMDResolveHandle.reset();
  145  
  146  		if (status == ASYNC_DONE)
  147  			acceptResult(result);
  148  		else if (status == ASYNC_ERROR)
  149  			processError(httpCode, statusString);
  150  	}
  151  
  152  	return mStatus;
  153  }
  154  
  155  void ThreadedScraper::processError(int status, const std::string statusString)
  156  {
  157  	if (status == HttpReq::REQ_430_TOOMANYSCRAPS || status == HttpReq::REQ_430_TOOMANYFAILURES || 
  158  		status == HttpReq::REQ_426_BLACKLISTED || status == HttpReq::REQ_FILESTREAM_ERROR || status == HttpReq::REQ_426_SERVERMAINTENANCE ||
  159  		status == HttpReq::REQ_403_BADLOGIN || status == HttpReq::REQ_401_FORBIDDEN)
  160  	{
  161  		// The whole run is refused: the page's outcome carries the
  162  		// scraper's words (one surface for one event, es-native-ui.md),
  163  		// where upstream raised a dialog over its card.
  164  		mExitCode = ASYNC_ERROR;
  165  		std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sProgressMutex);
  166  		sProgress.failed = true;
  167  		sProgress.failure = statusString;
  168  	}
  169  	else
  170  	{
  171  		mErrors.push_back(statusString);
  172  		std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sProgressMutex);
  173  		sProgress.errors = (int) mErrors.size();
  174  	}
  175  }
  176  
  177  void ThreadedScraper::run()
  178  {
  179  	while (mExitCode == ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS)
  180  	{
  181  		if (mPaused)
  182  		{
  183  			while (mExitCode == ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS && mPaused)
  184  			{
  185  				std::this_thread::yield();
  186  				std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(500));
  187  			}
  188  		}
  189  		
  190  		for (auto iter = mScraperThreads.cbegin(); iter != mScraperThreads.cend(); ++iter)
  191  		{
  192  			if (mExitCode != ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS)
  193  				break;
  194  
  195  			auto mScraperThread = *iter;
  196  
  197  			int state = mScraperThread->updateState();
  198  			switch (state)
  199  			{
  200  			case ASYNC_DONE:
  201  				acceptResult(*mScraperThread);
  202  				break;
  203  
  204  			case ASYNC_ERROR:
  205  				processError(mScraperThread->getError(), mScraperThread->getErrorString());
  206  				break;
  207  
  208  			default:
  209  				//std::this_thread::yield();
  210  				// std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
  211  				break;
  212  			}
  213  
  214  			if (mExitCode == ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS && state != ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS)
  215  			{
  216  				if (!mSearchQueue.empty())
  217  					ProcessNextGame(mScraperThread);
  218  				else
  219  				{					
  220  					mScraperThreads.erase(iter);
  221  					if (mScraperThreads.size() == 0)
  222  					{
  223  						mExitCode = ASYNC_DONE;
  224  						LOG(LogDebug) << "ThreadedScraper::finished";
  225  					}
  226  					break;
  227  				}
  228  			}
  229  		}
  230  	}
  231  	
  232  	{
  233  		// How it ended, for the page: the elapsed time freezes here.
  234  		std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sProgressMutex);
  235  		sProgress.finished = true;
  236  		sProgress.elapsedMs = (int) std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - sStarted).count();
  237  		LOG(LogInfo) << "ThreadedScraper: finished " << sProgress.done << " of " << sProgress.total
  238  			<< " (errors " << sProgress.errors << (sProgress.cancelled ? ", cancelled" : "") << (sProgress.failed ? ", failed" : "") << ")";
  239  	}
  240  
  241  	delete this;
  242  	ThreadedScraper::mInstance = nullptr;
  243  }
  244  
  245  // Called as each game is handed to a thread: the games finished so far are
  246  // the ones neither queued nor in a thread's hands, and the game named is
  247  // the one just handed out.
  248  void ThreadedScraper::updateUI()
  249  {
  250  	int done = mTotal - (int) mSearchQueue.size() - (int) mScraperThreads.size();
  251  	if (done < 0)
  252  		done = 0;
  253  
  254  	std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sProgressMutex);
  255  	sProgress.done = done;
  256  	sProgress.total = mTotal;
  257  	sProgress.game = mCurrentGame;
  258  }
  259  
  260  void ThreadedScraper::acceptResult(ScraperThread& thread)
  261  {
  262  	LOG(LogDebug) << "ThreadedScraper::acceptResult >>";
  263  
  264  	ScraperSearchResult& result = thread.getResult();
  265  	if (result.mdl.getName().empty())
  266  	{		
  267  		auto scraperName = Scraper::getScraperName(Scraper::getScraper());
  268  		thread.getSearchParams().game->getMetadata().setScrapeDate(scraperName);
  269  		return;
  270  	}
  271  
  272  	ScraperSearchParams& search = thread.getSearchParams();
  273  	auto game = search.game;
  274  
  275  	mWindow->postToUiThread([game, result]()
  276  	{
  277  		LOG(LogDebug) << "ThreadedScraper::importScrappedMetadata";
  278  		game->importP2k(result.p2k);
  279  		game->getMetadata().importScrappedMetadata(result.mdl);
  280  		game->detectLanguageAndRegion(true);
  281  		game->getMetadata().setScrapeDate(result.scraper);
  282  
  283  		LOG(LogDebug) << "ThreadedScraper::saveToGamelistRecovery";
  284  		saveToGamelistRecovery(game);
  285  	});
  286  
  287  	LOG(LogDebug) << "ThreadedScraper::acceptResult <<";
  288  }
  289  
  290  void ThreadedScraper::start(Window* window, const std::queue<ScraperSearchParams>& searches)
  291  {
  292  	if (ThreadedScraper::mInstance != nullptr)
  293  		return;
  294  
  295  	std::string error;
  296  	int threadCount = Scraper::getScraper()->getThreadCount(error);
  297  	if (threadCount < 0)
  298  	{
  299  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("AN ERROR OCCURRED") + std::string(" :\r\n") + error));
  300  		return;
  301  	}
  302  
  303  	if (threadCount == 0)
  304  		threadCount = 1;
  305  
  306  	ThreadedScraper::mInstance = new ThreadedScraper(window, searches, threadCount);
  307  
  308  	try
  309  	{
  310  		ThreadedScraper::mInstance->Process();
  311  	}
  312  	catch (const std::exception& e)
  313  	{
  314  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("AN ERROR OCCURRED") + std::string(" :\r\n") + e.what()));
  315  		delete ThreadedScraper::mInstance;
  316  		ThreadedScraper::mInstance = nullptr;
  317  	}
  318  }
  319  
  320  // The player's CANCEL (D-UI-078): the run's loop ends at its next turn and
  321  // what was scraped stays scraped. Marked before the loop is told, so run()
  322  // finds it set however quickly it ends.
  323  void ThreadedScraper::stop()
  324  {
  325  	auto thread = ThreadedScraper::mInstance;
  326  	if (thread == nullptr)
  327  		return;
  328  
  329  	{
  330  		std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(sProgressMutex);
  331  		sProgress.cancelled = true;
  332  	}
  333  	try
  334  	{
  335  		thread->mExitCode = ASYNC_DONE;
  336  	}
  337  	catch (...) {}
  338  }
  339  
```

```text
   48  
   49  // The scrape runs here, on threads of its own, and reports to a page
   50  // (GuiScraperRun) through progress() -- the fourth surface tier
   51  // (es-native-ui.md, D-UI-078, fork #241): a page that owns the screen for
   52  // the run's length, whose one way out while it runs is CANCEL (stop()).
   53  // Upstream drew the run on a corner card the player could play through and
   54  // ended it with a toast; a scrape is minutes for a library, and a job that
   55  // long gets a page, not a card.
   56  class ThreadedScraper
   57  {
   58  public:
   59  	// The run as a page reads it: what it is on, what it has done, how it
   60  	// ended. Copied out under a lock; the run's own counters stay with it.
   61  	struct Progress
   62  	{
   63  		bool running = false;     // a run is in flight, or ended and not yet let go
   64  		int done = 0;             // games finished, scraped or not
   65  		int total = 0;
   66  		std::string game;         // the one being looked up now
   67  		int errors = 0;           // games the scraper could not do (a miss, a timeout)
   68  		bool finished = false;
   69  		bool cancelled = false;   // stop(): the player's word for the outcome
   70  		bool failed = false;      // the scraper refused the whole run (a bad login, a ban)
   71  		std::string failure;      // its words, when it did
   72  		int elapsedMs = 0;        // since the run began; frozen once finished
   73  	};
   74  
   75  	static void start(Window* window, const std::queue<ScraperSearchParams>& searches);
   76  	static void stop();
   77  	static bool isRunning() { return mInstance != nullptr; }
   78  	static Progress progress();
   79  	
   80  	static void pause() { mPaused = true; }
   81  	static void resume() { mPaused = false; }
   82  
   83  	static std::string formatGameName(FileData* game);
   84  
   85  private:
   86  	ThreadedScraper(Window* window, const std::queue<ScraperSearchParams>& searches, int threadCount);
   87  	~ThreadedScraper();
   88  
   89  	void Process();
   90  	void ProcessNextGame(ScraperThread* thread);
   91  
   92  	Window* mWindow;
   93  	
   94  	std::string		mCurrentGame;
   95  
   96  	std::vector<std::string> mErrors;
   97
```

```text
  300  void DetailedContainer::createVideo()
  301  {
  302  	if (mVideo != nullptr)
  303  		return;
  304  
  305  	const float padding = 0.01f;
  306  	auto mSize = mParent->getSize();
  307

  309  	// Create the correct type of video window
  310  #ifdef _RPI_
  311  	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("VideoOmxPlayer"))
  312  		mVideo = new VideoPlayerComponent(mWindow, "");
  313  	else

  315  		mVideo = new VideoVlcComponent(mWindow);

  318  	mVideo->setSnapshotSource(IMAGE);

  321  	mVideo->setPosition(mSize.x() * 0.25f, mSize.y() * 0.4f);
  322  	mVideo->setSize(mSize.x() * (0.5f - 2 * padding), mSize.y() * 0.4f);
  323  	mVideo->setStartDelay(2000);
  324  	mVideo->setDefaultZIndex(31);
  325  	mParent->addChild(mVideo);

  328  void DetailedContainer::initMDLabels()
  329  {
  330  	auto mSize = mParent->getSize();
  331  	std::shared_ptr<Font> defaultFont = Font::get(FONT_SIZE_SMALL);
  332  
  333  	auto components = getMetaComponents();
  334  
  335  	const unsigned int colCount = 2;
  336  	const unsigned int rowCount = (int)(components.size() / 2);
  337  
  338  	Vector3f start(mSize.x() * 0.01f, mSize.y() * 0.625f, 0.0f);
  339  
  340  	const float colSize = (mSize.x() * 0.48f) / colCount;
  341  	const float rowPadding = 0.01f * mSize.y();
  342  
  343  	int i = 0;	
  344  	for (unsigned int i = 0; i < components.size(); i++)
  345  	{
  346  		if (components[i].labelid.empty() || components[i].label == nullptr)
  347  			continue;
  348  
  349  		const unsigned int row = i % rowCount;
  350  		Vector3f pos(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
  351  		if (row == 0)
  352  			pos = start + Vector3f(colSize * (i / rowCount), 0, 0);
  353  		else 
  354  		{
  355  			// work from the last component
  356  			GuiComponent* lc = components[i - 1].label;
  357  			pos = lc->getPosition() + Vector3f(0, lc->getSize().y() + rowPadding, 0);
  358  		}
  359  
  360  		components[i].label->setFont(defaultFont);
  361  		components[i].label->setPosition(pos);
  362  		components[i].label->setDefaultZIndex(40);
  363  	}
  364  }
  365  
  366  void DetailedContainer::initMDValues()
  367  {
  368  	auto mSize = mParent->getSize();
  369  
  370  	std::shared_ptr<Font> defaultFont = Font::get(FONT_SIZE_SMALL);
  371  	mRating.setSize(defaultFont->getHeight() * 5.0f, (float)defaultFont->getHeight());
  372  
  373  	float bottom = 0.0f;
  374  
  375  	const float colSize = (mSize.x() * 0.48f) / 2;
  376  
  377  	auto components = getMetaComponents();
  378  	for (auto mdc : components)
  379  	{
  380  		TextComponent* text = dynamic_cast<TextComponent*>(mdc.component);
  381  		if (text != nullptr)
  382  			text->setFont(defaultFont);
  383  		
  384  		if (mdc.labelid.empty() || mdc.label == nullptr)
  385  			continue;
  386  
  387  		const float heightDiff = (mdc.label->getSize().y() - mdc.label->getSize().y()) / 2;
  388  		mdc.component->setPosition(mdc.label->getPosition() + Vector3f(mdc.label->getSize().x(), heightDiff, 0));
  389  		mdc.component->setSize(colSize - mdc.label->getSize().x(), mdc.component->getSize().y());
  390  		mdc.component->setDefaultZIndex(40);
  391  
  392  		float testBot = mdc.component->getPosition().y() + mdc.component->getSize().y();
  393  		if (testBot > bottom)
  394  			bottom = testBot;
  395  	}
  396  
  397  	mDescription.setPosition(mDescription.getPosition().x(), bottom + mSize.y() * 0.01f);
  398  	mDescription.setSize(mDescription.getSize().x(), mSize.y() - mDescription.getPosition().y());
  399  }
  400  
  401  void DetailedContainer::loadIfThemed(ImageComponent** pImage, const std::shared_ptr<ThemeData>& theme, const std::string& element, bool forceLoad, bool loadPath)
  402  {
  403  	using namespace ThemeFlags;
  404  
  405  	auto elem = theme->getElement(getName(), element, "image");
  406  
  407  	if (forceLoad || (elem && elem->properties.size() > 0 && (!elem->has("visible") || elem->get<bool>("visible"))))
  408  	{
  409  		createImageComponent(pImage, element == "md_fanart", element == "md_fanart" && Settings::AllImagesAsync());			
  410  		(*pImage)->applyTheme(theme, getName(), element, loadPath ? ALL : ALL ^ (PATH));	
  411  	}
  412  	else if ((*pImage) != nullptr)
  413  	{
  414  		removeChild(*pImage);
  415  		delete (*pImage);
  416  		(*pImage) = nullptr;
  417  	}
  418  }
  419  
  420  void DetailedContainer::onThemeChanged(const std::shared_ptr<ThemeData>& theme)
  421  {
  422  	using namespace ThemeFlags;
  423  	
  424  	std::string viewName = getName();
  425  
  426  	if (theme != mCustomTheme)
  427  	{
  428  		mTheme = theme;
  429  
  430  		const ThemeData::ThemeElement* elem = theme->getElement(viewName, "gameextras", "gameextras");
  431  
  432  		if (elem && elem->has("path_binding"))
  433  		{
  434  			mPerGameExtrasPath = elem->get<std::string>("path_binding");
  435  			mIsPerGameExtrasPathBinding = true;
  436  		}
  437  		else if (elem && elem->has("path"))
  438  		{
  439  			mIsPerGameExtrasPathBinding = false;
  440  			mPerGameExtrasPath = elem->get<std::string>("path");
  441  		}
  442  		else					
  443  			mPerGameExtrasPath = "";
  444  
  445  		initMDLabels();
  446  		initMDValues();
  447  	}
  448  	
  449  	mName.applyTheme(theme, viewName, "md_name", ALL);
  450  
  451  	auto velem = theme->getElement(viewName, "md_video", "video");
  452  	if (velem && (!velem->has("visible") || velem->get<bool>("visible")))
  453  	{
  454  		createVideo();
  455  		mVideo->applyTheme(theme, viewName, "md_video", ALL ^ (PATH));
  456  	}
  457  	else if (mVideo != nullptr)
  458  	{
  459  		removeChild(mVideo);
  460  		delete mVideo;
  461  		mVideo = nullptr;
  462  	}
  463  
  464  	loadIfThemed(&mImage, theme, "md_image", (mVideo == nullptr && mViewType == DetailedContainerType::DetailedView));
  465  	loadIfThemed(&mThumbnail, theme, "md_thumbnail");
  466  	loadIfThemed(&mFlag, theme, "md_flag");
  467  
  468  	for (auto& md : mdImages)
  469  		loadIfThemed(&md.component, theme, md.id);
  470  
  471  	loadIfThemed(&mKidGame, theme, "md_kidgame", false, true);
  472  	loadIfThemed(&mNotKidGame, theme, "md_notkidgame", false, true);
  473  
  474  	loadIfThemed(&mGunGame, theme, "md_gungame", false, true);
  475  	loadIfThemed(&mNotGunGame, theme, "md_notgungame", false, true);
  476  
  477  	loadIfThemed(&mWheelGame, theme, "md_wheelgame", false, true);
  478  	loadIfThemed(&mNotWheelGame, theme, "md_notwheelgame", false, true);
  479  
  480  	loadIfThemed(&mTrackballGame, theme, "md_trackballgame", false, true);
  481  	loadIfThemed(&mNotTrackballGame, theme, "md_nottrackballgame", false, true);
  482  
  483  	loadIfThemed(&mSpinnerGame, theme, "md_spinnergame", false, true);
  484  	loadIfThemed(&mNotSpinnerGame, theme, "md_notspinnergame", false, true);
  485  
  486  	loadIfThemed(&mCheevos, theme, "md_cheevos", false, true);
  487  	loadIfThemed(&mNotCheevos, theme, "md_notcheevos", false, true);
  488  
  489  	loadIfThemed(&mNetplay, theme, "md_netplay", false, true);
  490  	loadIfThemed(&mNotNetplay, theme, "md_notnetplay", false, true);
  491  	
  492  	loadIfThemed(&mFavorite, theme, "md_favorite", false, true);
  493  	loadIfThemed(&mNotFavorite, theme, "md_notfavorite", false, true);
  494  	
  495  	loadIfThemed(&mHidden, theme, "md_hidden", false, true);
  496  	loadIfThemed(&mManual, theme, "md_manual", false, true);
  497  	loadIfThemed(&mNoManual, theme, "md_nomanual", false, true);	
  498  	loadIfThemed(&mMap, theme, "md_map", false, true);
  499  	loadIfThemed(&mNoMap, theme, "md_nomap", false, true);
  500  	loadIfThemed(&mSaveState, theme, "md_savestate", false, true);
  501  	loadIfThemed(&mNoSaveState, theme, "md_nosavestate", false, true);		
  502  	
  503  	for (auto comp : getComponents())
  504  		disableComponent(comp);
  505  
  506  	for (auto ctrl : getMetaComponents())
  507  	{
  508  		if (ctrl.label != nullptr && theme->getElement(viewName, ctrl.labelid, "text"))
  509  			ctrl.label->applyTheme(theme, viewName, ctrl.labelid, ALL);
  510  
  511  		if (ctrl.component != nullptr && theme->getElement(viewName, ctrl.id, ctrl.expectedType))
  512  			ctrl.component->applyTheme(theme, viewName, ctrl.id, ALL);
  513  	}
  514  
  515  	if (theme->getElement(viewName, "md_description", "text"))
  516  	{
  517  		mDescription.applyTheme(theme, viewName, "md_description", ALL);
  518  		mDescription.setAutoScroll(TextComponent::AutoScrollType::VERTICAL);
  519  
  520  		if (!isChild(&mDescription))
  521  			addChild(&mDescription);
  522  	}
  523  	else if (mViewType == DetailedContainerType::GridView)
  524  		removeChild(&mDescription);
  525  
  526  	// Add new theme extras
  527  	for (auto extra : mThemeExtras)
  528  	{
  529  		removeChild(extra);
  530  		delete extra;
  531  	}
  532  
  533  	mThemeExtras.clear();
  534  	mThemeExtras = ThemeData::makeExtras(theme, viewName, mWindow, false, (ThemeData::ExtraImportType) (ThemeData::ExtraImportType::WITH_ACTIVATESTORYBOARD | ThemeData::ExtraImportType::PERGAMEEXTRAS));
  535  	for (auto extra : mThemeExtras)
  536  		addChild(extra);
  537  
  538  	mParent->sortChildren();
  539  }

  541  Vector3f DetailedContainer::getLaunchTarget()
  542  {
  543  	Vector3f target(Renderer::getScreenWidth() / 2.0f, Renderer::getScreenHeight() / 2.0f, 0);
  544  
  545  	if (mVideo != nullptr)
  546  		target = Vector3f(mVideo->getCenter().x(), mVideo->getCenter().y(), 0);
  547  	else if (mImage != nullptr && mImage->hasImage())
  548  		target = Vector3f(mImage->getCenter().x(), mImage->getCenter().y(), 0);
  549  	else if (mThumbnail != nullptr && mThumbnail->hasImage())
  550  		target = Vector3f(mThumbnail->getCenter().x(), mThumbnail->getCenter().y(), 0);
  551  
  552  	return target;
  553  }
  554  
  555  #define GRIDPADDING Renderer::getScreenHeight() * 0.004f
  556  
  557  void DetailedContainer::createFolderGrid(Vector2f targetSize, std::vector<std::string> thumbs)
  558  {
  559  	if (thumbs.size() == 0)
  560  		return;
  561  
  562  	auto gridSize = Vector2i(3, 2);
  563  	mFolderView = new ComponentGrid(mWindow, gridSize);
  564  
  565  	auto sz = Vector2f(targetSize.x() / (float)gridSize.x(), targetSize.y() / (float)gridSize.y());
  566  
  567  	int idx = 0;
  568  	for (int x = 0; x < gridSize.x(); x++)
  569  	{
  570  		for (int y = 0; y < gridSize.y(); y++)
  571  		{
  572  			if (idx >= thumbs.size())
  573  				break;
  574  
  575  			auto image = std::make_shared<ImageComponent>(mWindow);
  576  			image->setMaxSize(sz);
  577  			image->setIsLinear(true);
  578  			image->setImage(thumbs[idx]);
  579  			image->setPadding(Vector4f(GRIDPADDING, GRIDPADDING, GRIDPADDING, GRIDPADDING));
  580  			mFolderView->setEntry(image, Vector2i(x, y), false, false);
  581  			idx++;
  582  		}
  583  	}
  584  
  585  	mFolderView->setSize(targetSize);
  586  }
  587  
  588  void DetailedContainer::updateFolderViewAmbiantProperties()
  589  {
  590  	if (mFolderView != nullptr)
  591  	{
  592  		auto parent = mFolderView->getParent();
  593  		if (parent != nullptr)
  594  		{
  595  			auto opacity = parent->getOpacity();
  596  
  597  			for (int i = 0; i < mFolderView->getChildCount(); i++)
  598  				mFolderView->setOpacity(opacity);
  599  		}
  600  	}
  601  }
  602  
  603  
  604  void DetailedContainer::updateDetailsForFolder(FolderData* folder)
  605  {
  606  	auto games = folder->getChildrenListToDisplay();
  607  	if (games.size() == 0)
  608  		return;
  609  	
  610  	auto desc = folder->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Desc);
  611  	if (desc.empty())
  612  	{
  613  		std::vector<std::string> gamesList;
  614  
  615  		for (auto child : games)
  616  		{
  617  			gamesList.push_back("- " + child->getName());
  618  			if (gamesList.size() == 6)
  619  				break;
  620  		}
  621  
  622  		desc = "\n" + Utils::String::join(gamesList, "\n");
  623  
  624  		int count = games.size();
  625  
  626  		char trstring[2048];
  627  		snprintf(trstring, 2048, ngettext(
  628  			"This folder contains %i game, including: %s",
  629  			"This folder contains %i games, including: %s", count), count, desc.c_str());
  630  
  631  		mDescription.setText(trstring);
  632  	}	
  633  
  634  	FileData* firstGameWithImage = nullptr;
  635  
  636  	std::vector<std::string> thumbs;
  637  	for (auto child : games)
  638  	{
  639  		if (!child->getThumbnailPath().empty())
  640  			thumbs.push_back(child->getThumbnailPath());
  641  
  642  		if (firstGameWithImage == nullptr && !child->getImagePath().empty())
  643  			firstGameWithImage = child;		
  644  	}
  645  
  646  
  647  
  648  	if (firstGameWithImage != nullptr)
  649  	{
  650  		std::string imagePath = firstGameWithImage->getImagePath().empty() ? firstGameWithImage->getThumbnailPath() : firstGameWithImage->getImagePath();
  651  		
  652  		if (mVideo != nullptr && mVideo->showSnapshots())
  653  		{
  654  			mVideo->setImage(":/blank.png");
  655  			mVideo->setVideo("");
  656  
  657  			createFolderGrid(mVideo->getTargetSize(), thumbs);
  658  			if (mFolderView)
  659  				mVideo->addChild(mFolderView);
  660  			else
  661  			{
  662  				std::string snapShot = imagePath;
  663  
  664  				auto src = mVideo->getSnapshotSource();
  665  
  666  
  667  				if (src == TITLESHOT && Utils::FileSystem::exists(firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::TitleShot)))
  668  					snapShot = firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::TitleShot);
  669  				else if (src == BOXART && Utils::FileSystem::exists(firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::BoxArt)))
  670  					snapShot = firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::BoxArt);
  671  				else if (src == MARQUEE && !firstGameWithImage->getMarqueePath().empty())
  672  					snapShot = firstGameWithImage->getMarqueePath();
  673  				else if ((src == THUMBNAIL || src == BOXART) && !firstGameWithImage->getThumbnailPath().empty())
  674  					snapShot = firstGameWithImage->getThumbnailPath();
  675  				else if ((src == IMAGE || src == TITLESHOT) && !firstGameWithImage->getImagePath().empty())
  676  					snapShot = firstGameWithImage->getImagePath();
  677  				else if (src == FANART && Utils::FileSystem::exists(firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::FanArt)))
  678  					snapShot = firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::FanArt);
  679  				else if (src == CARTRIDGE && Utils::FileSystem::exists(firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Cartridge)))
  680  					snapShot = firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Cartridge);
  681  				else if (src == MIX && Utils::FileSystem::exists(firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Mix)))
  682  					snapShot = firstGameWithImage->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Mix);
  683  
  684  				mVideo->setImage(snapShot);
  685  			}
  686  		}
  687  		
  688  		if (mThumbnail != nullptr)
  689  		{
  690  			if (mViewType == DetailedContainerType::VideoView && mImage != nullptr && !mImage->hasImage())
  691  				mImage->setImage(firstGameWithImage->getImagePath(), false, mImage->getMaxSizeInfo());
  692  
  693  			if (!mThumbnail->hasImage())
  694  				mThumbnail->setImage(firstGameWithImage->getThumbnailPath(), false, mThumbnail->getMaxSizeInfo());
  695  		}
  696  
  697  		if (mImage != nullptr && !mImage->hasImage())
  698  		{
  699  			if (mVideo == nullptr || !mVideo->showSnapshots())
  700  			{
  701  				mImage->setImage(":/blank.png");
  702  
  703  				createFolderGrid(mImage->getSize(), thumbs);
  704  				if (mFolderView)
  705  					mImage->addChild(mFolderView);
  706  			}
  707  			else
  708  			{
  709  				if (mViewType == DetailedContainerType::VideoView && mThumbnail == nullptr)
  710  					mImage->setImage(firstGameWithImage->getThumbnailPath(), false, mImage->getMaxSizeInfo());
  711  				else if (mViewType != DetailedContainerType::VideoView)
  712  					mImage->setImage(imagePath, false, mImage->getMaxSizeInfo());
  713  			}
  714  		}
  715  	}
  716  
  717  	mReleaseDate.setValue(folder->getMetadata(MetaDataId::ReleaseDate));
  718  	mDeveloper.setValue(folder->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Developer));
  719  	mPublisher.setValue(folder->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Publisher));
  720  	mGenre.setValue(folder->getGenre());
  721  	mLastPlayed.setValue("");
  722  	mPlayCount.setValue("");
  723  	mGameTime.setValue("");
  724  }
  725  
  726  void DetailedContainer::resetThemedExtras()
  727  {
  728  	if (mCustomTheme != nullptr)
  729  	{
  730  		mCustomTheme = nullptr;
  731  
  732  		if (mTheme != nullptr)
  733  			onThemeChanged(mTheme);
  734  	}
  735  }
  736  
  737  void DetailedContainer::loadThemedExtras(FileData* file)
  738  {
  739  	if (mPerGameExtrasPath.empty())
  740  	{
  741  		resetThemedExtras();			
  742  		return;
  743  	}
  744  	
  745  	std::string rootPath = mPerGameExtrasPath;
  746  
  747  	if (mIsPerGameExtrasPathBinding)
  748  	{
  749  		std::string evaluationResult = BindingManager::evaluateBindableExpression(mPerGameExtrasPath, file);		
  750  		if (evaluationResult.size())
  751  			rootPath = Utils::FileSystem::getCanonicalPath(evaluationResult);
  752  	}
  753  
  754  	std::string path;
  755  
  756  	if (Utils::String::endsWith(rootPath, ".xml") && Utils::FileSystem::exists(rootPath))
  757  		path = rootPath;
  758  	else
  759  	{
  760  		path = Utils::FileSystem::combine(rootPath, Utils::FileSystem::getStem(file->getPath()) + ".xml");
  761  
  762  		if (!Utils::FileSystem::exists(path) && !file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Crc32).empty())
  763  			path = Utils::FileSystem::combine(rootPath, file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Crc32) + ".xml");
  764  
  765  		if (!Utils::FileSystem::exists(path))
  766  			path = Utils::FileSystem::combine(rootPath, Utils::FileSystem::getStem(file->getPath()) + "/theme.xml");
  767  
  768  		if (!Utils::FileSystem::exists(path) && !file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Crc32).empty())
  769  			path = Utils::FileSystem::combine(rootPath, file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Crc32) + "/theme.xml");
  770  
  771  		if (!Utils::FileSystem::exists(path))
  772  		{
  773  			resetThemedExtras();
  774  			return;
  775  		}
  776  	}
  777  
  778  	auto customTheme = mTheme->clone(getName());
  779  	if (customTheme->appendFile(path, true))
  780  	{
  781  		auto currentTheme = mTheme;
  782  		mCustomTheme = customTheme;
  783  		onThemeChanged(mCustomTheme);
  784  		mTheme = currentTheme;
  785  	}
  786  	else
  787  		resetThemedExtras();
  788  }
  789  
  790  // Set the display aspect on every image under comp whose path is the
  791  // file's image (fork #243), and clear it on the others.
  792  void DetailedContainer::updateControls(FileData* file, bool isClearing, int moveBy, bool isDeactivating)
  793  {
  794  	bool state = (file != NULL);
  795  	if (state)
  796  	{
  797  		if (!isClearing && !isDeactivating)
  798  			loadThemedExtras(file);
  799  
  800  		if (mFolderView)
  801  		{
  802  			delete mFolderView;
  803  			mFolderView = nullptr;
  804  		}
  805  
  806  		std::string imagePath = file->getImagePath().empty() ? file->getThumbnailPath() : file->getImagePath();
  807  
  808  		if (mVideo != nullptr)
  809  		{
  810  			if (!mVideo->setVideo(file->getVideoPath()))
  811  				mVideo->setDefaultVideo();
  812  
  813  			std::string snapShot = imagePath;
  814  
  815  			auto src = mVideo->getSnapshotSource();
  816  
  817  			if (src == TITLESHOT && Utils::FileSystem::exists(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::TitleShot)))
  818  				snapShot = file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::TitleShot);
  819  			else if (src == BOXART && Utils::FileSystem::exists(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::BoxArt)))
  820  				snapShot = file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::BoxArt);
  821  			else if (src == MARQUEE && !file->getMarqueePath().empty())
  822  				snapShot = file->getMarqueePath();
  823  			else if ((src == THUMBNAIL || src == BOXART) && !file->getThumbnailPath().empty())
  824  				snapShot = file->getThumbnailPath();
  825  			else if ((src == IMAGE || src == TITLESHOT) && !file->getImagePath().empty())
  826  				snapShot = file->getImagePath();
  827  			else if (src == FANART && Utils::FileSystem::exists(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::FanArt)))
  828  				snapShot = file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::FanArt);
  829  			else if (src == CARTRIDGE && Utils::FileSystem::exists(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Cartridge)))
  830  				snapShot = file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Cartridge);
  831  			else if (src == MIX && Utils::FileSystem::exists(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Mix)))
  832  				snapShot = file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Mix);
  833  
  834  			mVideo->setImage(snapShot, false, mVideo->getMaxSizeInfo());
  835  		}
  836  
  837  		if (mThumbnail != nullptr)
  838  		{
  839  			if (mViewType == DetailedContainerType::VideoView && mImage != nullptr)
  840  				mImage->setImage(file->getImagePath(), false, mImage->getMaxSizeInfo());
  841  
  842  			mThumbnail->setImage(file->getThumbnailPath(), false, mThumbnail->getMaxSizeInfo());
  843  		}
  844  
  845  		if (mImage != nullptr)
  846  		{
  847  			if (mViewType == DetailedContainerType::VideoView && mThumbnail == nullptr)
  848  				mImage->setImage(file->getThumbnailPath(), false, mImage->getMaxSizeInfo());
  849  			else if (mViewType != DetailedContainerType::VideoView)
  850  				mImage->setImage(imagePath, false, mImage->getMaxSizeInfo());
  851  		}
  852  
  853  		for (auto& md : mdImages)
  854  		{
  855  			if (md.component != nullptr)
  856  			{
  857  				std::string image;
  858  
  859  				for (auto& id : md.metaDataIds)
  860  				{
  861  					if (id == MetaDataId::Marquee)
  862  					{
  863  						if (Utils::FileSystem::exists(file->getMarqueePath()))
  864  						{
  865  							image = file->getMarqueePath();
  866  							break;
  867  						}
  868  
  869  						continue;
  870  					}
  871  
  872  					std::string path = file->getMetadata(id);
  873  					if (Utils::FileSystem::exists(path))
  874  					{
  875  						image = path;
  876  						break;
  877  					}
  878  				}
  879  
  880  				if (!image.empty())
  881  					md.component->setImage(image, false, md.component->getMaxSizeInfo());
  882  				else
  883  					md.component->setImage("");
  884  			}
  885  		}
  886  
  887  		if (mFlag != nullptr)
  888  		{
  889  			if (file->getType() == GAME)
  890  			{
  891  				file->detectLanguageAndRegion(false);
  892  				mFlag->setImage(":/flags/" + LangInfo::getFlag(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Language), file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Region)) + ".png"
  893  					, false, mFlag->getMaxSizeInfo());
  894  			}
  895  			else
  896  				mFlag->setImage(":/folder.svg");
  897  		}
  898  
  899  		if (mManual != nullptr || mNoManual != nullptr)
  900  		{
  901  			bool hasManualOrMagazine = Settings::getInstance()->getBool("PreloadMedias") ?
  902  				!file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Manual).empty() || !file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Magazine).empty() :
  903  				Utils::FileSystem::exists(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Manual)) || Utils::FileSystem::exists(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Magazine));
  904  
  905  			if (mManual != nullptr)
  906  				mManual->setVisible(hasManualOrMagazine);
  907  
  908  			if (mNoManual != nullptr)
  909  				mNoManual->setVisible(!hasManualOrMagazine);
  910  		}
  911  
  912  		if (mMap != nullptr || mNoMap != nullptr)
  913  		{
  914  			bool hasMap = Settings::getInstance()->getBool("PreloadMedias") ?
  915  				!file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Map).empty() :
  916  				Utils::FileSystem::exists(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Map));
  917  
  918  			if (mMap != nullptr)
  919  				mMap->setVisible(hasMap);
  920  
  921  			if (mNoMap != nullptr)
  922  				mNoMap->setVisible(!hasMap);
  923  		}
  924  
  925  		// Save states
  926  		bool hasSaveState = false;
  927  		bool systemSupportsSaveStates = SaveStateRepository::isEnabled(file);
  928  
  929  		if (systemSupportsSaveStates && (mSaveState != nullptr || mNoSaveState != nullptr))
  930  			hasSaveState = file->getSourceFileData()->getSystem()->getSaveStateRepository()->hasSaveStates(file);
  931  
  932  		if (mSaveState != nullptr)
  933  			mSaveState->setVisible(systemSupportsSaveStates && hasSaveState);
  934  
  935  		if (mNoSaveState != nullptr)
  936  			mNoSaveState->setVisible(systemSupportsSaveStates && !hasSaveState);
  937  
  938  		// Kid game
  939  		if (mKidGame != nullptr)
  940  			mKidGame->setVisible(file->getKidGame());
  941  
  942  		if (mNotKidGame != nullptr)
  943  			mNotKidGame->setVisible(!file->getKidGame());
  944  
  945  		// Gun game
  946  		if (mGunGame != nullptr)
  947  			mGunGame->setVisible(file->isLightGunGame());
  948  
  949  		if (mNotGunGame != nullptr)
  950  			mNotGunGame->setVisible(!file->isLightGunGame());
  951  
  952  		// Wheel game
  953  		if (mWheelGame != nullptr)
  954  			mWheelGame->setVisible(file->isWheelGame());
  955  
  956  		if (mNotWheelGame != nullptr)
  957  			mNotWheelGame->setVisible(!file->isWheelGame());
  958  
  959  		// Trackball game
  960  		if (mTrackballGame != nullptr)
  961  			mTrackballGame->setVisible(file->isTrackballGame());
  962  
  963  		if (mNotTrackballGame != nullptr)
  964  			mNotTrackballGame->setVisible(!file->isTrackballGame());
  965  
  966  		// Spinner game
  967  		if (mSpinnerGame != nullptr)
  968  			mSpinnerGame->setVisible(file->isSpinnerGame());
  969  
  970  		if (mNotSpinnerGame != nullptr)
  971  			mNotSpinnerGame->setVisible(!file->isSpinnerGame());
  972  
  973  		bool systemHasCheevos = 
  974  			SystemConf::getInstance()->getBool("global.retroachievements") && (
  975  			file->getSourceFileData()->getSystem()->isCheevosSupported() || 
  976  			file->getSystem()->isCollection() || 
  977  			!file->getSystem()->isGameSystem()); // Fake cheevos supported if the game is in a collection cuz there are lot of games from different systems
  978  
  979  		// Cheevos
  980  		if (mCheevos != nullptr)
  981  			mCheevos->setVisible(systemHasCheevos && file->hasCheevos());
  982  
  983  		if (mNotCheevos != nullptr)
  984  			mNotCheevos->setVisible(systemHasCheevos && !file->hasCheevos());
  985  
  986  		// Netplay
  987  		bool systemHasNetplay =
  988  			SystemConf::getInstance()->getBool("global.netplay") && (
  989  				file->getSourceFileData()->getSystem()->isNetplaySupported() ||
  990  				file->getSystem()->isCollection() ||
  991  				!file->getSystem()->isGameSystem());
  992  
  993  		if (mNetplay != nullptr)
  994  			mNetplay->setVisible(systemHasNetplay && file->isNetplaySupported());
  995  
  996  		if (mNotNetplay != nullptr)
  997  			mNotNetplay->setVisible(systemHasNetplay && !file->isNetplaySupported());
  998  
  999  		if (mFavorite != nullptr)
 1000  			mFavorite->setVisible(file->getFavorite());
 1001  
 1002  		if (mNotFavorite != nullptr)
 1003  			mNotFavorite->setVisible(!file->getFavorite());
 1004  		
 1005  		if (mHidden != nullptr)
 1006  			mHidden->setVisible(file->getHidden());
 1007  	
 1008  		mDescription.setText(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Desc));
 1009  
 1010  		auto valueOrUnknown = [](const std::string value) { return value.empty() ? _("Unknown") : value; };
 1011  		auto valueOrOne = [](const std::string value) 
 1012  		{ 
 1013  			auto split = value.rfind("+");
 1014  			if (split != std::string::npos)
 1015  				return value.substr(0, split);
 1016  
 1017  			split = value.rfind("-");
 1018  			if (split != std::string::npos)
 1019  				return value.substr(split + 1);
 1020  
 1021  			std::string ret = value;
 1022  
 1023  			int count = Utils::String::toInteger(value);
 1024  
 1025  			if (count >= 10) ret = "9";
 1026  			else if (count == 0) ret = "1";
 1027  
 1028  			return ret;
 1029  		};
 1030  
 1031  		mRating.setValue(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Rating));
 1032  		mReleaseDate.setValue(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::ReleaseDate));
 1033  		mDeveloper.setValue(valueOrUnknown(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Developer)));
 1034  		mPublisher.setValue(valueOrUnknown(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Publisher)));
 1035  		mGenre.setValue(valueOrUnknown(file->getGenre()));
 1036  
 1037  		if (mPlayers.getOriginalThemeText() == "1")
 1038  			mPlayers.setValue(valueOrOne(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Players)));
 1039  		else 
 1040  			mPlayers.setValue(valueOrUnknown(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Players)));
 1041  
 1042  		mName.setValue(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Name));
 1043  		mTextFavorite.setText(file->getFavorite()?_("YES"):_("NO"));
 1044  
 1045  		if (file->getType() == GAME)
 1046  		{
 1047  			mLastPlayed.setValue(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::LastPlayed));
 1048  			mPlayCount.setValue(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::PlayCount));
 1049  			mGameTime.setValue(Utils::Time::secondsToString(atol(file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::GameTime).c_str())));
 1050  		}
 1051  		else if (file->getType() == FOLDER)
 1052  			updateDetailsForFolder((FolderData*)file);
 1053  
 1054  		for (auto extra : mThemeExtras)
 1055  			BindingManager::updateBindings(extra, file);
 1056  
 1057  		// A screenshot under the SCREENSHOTS entry is drawn at the aspect
 1058  		// of the system it was taken in (fork #243, D-UI-080): RetroArch
 1059  		// wrote it at the core's native size. Only the pictures bound to
 1060  		// this file's image -- the theme's artwork element, the md_image
 1061  		// -- get the aspect; every other image keeps its own, and a file
 1062  		// of any other system resets them.
 1063  		const bool screenshots = file->getSystem() != nullptr && file->getSystem()->hasPlatformId(PlatformIds::IMAGEVIEWER);
 1064  		const DisplayAspect::Transform transform = screenshots ? DisplayAspect::forScreenshot(file) : DisplayAspect::Transform();
 1065  		const std::string imageBound = file->getImagePath();
 1066  		for (auto extra : mThemeExtras)
 1067  			DisplayAspect::applyToBoundImages(extra, imageBound, transform);
 1068  		if (mImage != nullptr)
 1069  		{
 1070  			mImage->setDisplayAspect(transform.aspect);
 1071  			mImage->setDisplayRotation(transform.turns);
 1072  		}
 1073  	}
 1074  
 1075  	if (state && file != nullptr && !isClearing)
 1076  	{
 1077  		for (auto comp : getComponents())
 1078  			handleStoryBoard(comp, true, moveBy);
 1079  	}
 1080  	
 1081  	// We're clearing / populating : don't setup fade animations
 1082  	if (file == nullptr && isClearing)
 1083  	{
 1084  		resetThemedExtras();
 1085  
 1086  		for (auto comp : getComponents())
 1087  		{
 1088  			comp->cancelAnimation(0);
 1089  			disableComponent(comp);
 1090  		}
 1091  
 1092  		return;
 1093  	}
 1094  
 1095  	if (state == mState)
 1096  		return;
 1097  
 1098  	mState = state;
 1099  
 1100  	bool fadeOut = !state;
 1101  
 1102  	std::vector<GuiComponent*> comps = getComponents();
 1103  	for (auto comp : comps)
 1104  	{
 1105  		if (fadeOut && isDeactivating && hasActivationStoryboard(comp, false, true))
 1106  		{
 1107  			handleStoryBoard(comp, false, moveBy);
 1108  		}
 1109  		else if (fadeOut && isDeactivating && !hasActivationStoryboard(comp))
 1110  		{
 1111  			comp->deselectStoryboard();
 1112  			comp->setOpacity(0);
 1113  			disableComponent(comp);
 1114  		}
 1115  		else
 1116  		{
 1117  			if (fadeOut)
 1118  				comp->enableStoryboardProperty("opacity", false);
 1119  			else
 1120  				comp->deselectStoryboard(false);
 1121  
 1122  			comp->cancelAnimation(0);
 1123  
 1124  			unsigned char initialOpacity = fadeOut ? comp->getOpacity() : 255;
 1125  
 1126  			auto func = [comp, initialOpacity](float t)
 1127  			{
 1128  				comp->setOpacity((unsigned char)(Math::lerp(0.0f, 1.0f, t) * initialOpacity)); // 255
 1129  			};
 1130  			
 1131  			comp->setAnimation(new LambdaAnimation(func, fadeOut && isDeactivating ? 500 : 250), 0, [this, comp, fadeOut, file]
 1132  			{
 1133  				if (fadeOut)
 1134  				{
 1135  					comp->enableStoryboardProperty("opacity", true);
 1136  					comp->deselectStoryboard(true);
 1137  					comp->setOpacity(0);
 1138  					disableComponent(comp);
 1139  				}
 1140  
 1141  			}, fadeOut);
 1142  		}
 1143  	}
 1144  
 1145  #ifdef _RPI_
 1146  	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("ScreenSaverOmxPlayer"))
 1147  		Utils::FileSystem::removeFile(getTitlePath());
 1148  #endif
 1149  }
 1150  
 1151  void DetailedContainer::handleStoryBoard(GuiComponent* comp, bool activate, int moveBy, bool recursive)
 1152  {
 1153  	if (moveBy > 0 && comp->storyBoardExists(activate ? "activateNext" : "deactivateNext"))
 1154  	{
 1155  		comp->deselectStoryboard(activate);
 1156  		comp->selectStoryboard(activate ? "activateNext" : "deactivateNext");
 1157  
 1158  		if (comp->isShowing())
 1159  			comp->startStoryboard();
 1160  	}
 1161  	else if (moveBy < 0 && comp->storyBoardExists(activate ? "activatePrev" : "deactivatePrev"))
 1162  	{
 1163  		comp->deselectStoryboard(activate);
 1164  		comp->selectStoryboard(activate ? "activatePrev" : "deactivatePrev");
 1165  
 1166  		if (comp->isShowing())
 1167  			comp->startStoryboard();
 1168  	}
 1169  	else if (moveBy != 0 && comp->storyBoardExists(activate ? "activate" : "deactivate"))
 1170  	{
 1171  		comp->deselectStoryboard(activate);
 1172  		comp->selectStoryboard(activate ? "activate" : "deactivate");
 1173  
 1174  		if (comp->isShowing())
 1175  			comp->startStoryboard();
 1176  	}
 1177  	else if (activate && moveBy == 0 && comp->storyBoardExists("open"))
 1178  	{
 1179  		comp->deselectStoryboard();
 1180  		comp->selectStoryboard("open");
 1181  
 1182  		if (comp->isShowing())
 1183  			comp->startStoryboard();
 1184  	}
 1185  	else if (activate && moveBy == 0 && comp->storyBoardExists("") && !comp->hasStoryBoard("", true))
 1186  	{
 1187  		comp->deselectStoryboard();
 1188  		comp->selectStoryboard();
 1189  
 1190  		if (comp->isShowing())
 1191  			comp->onShow();
 1192  	}
 1193  	else if (activate && moveBy == 0 && comp->storyBoardExists("activate"))
 1194  	{
 1195  		comp->deselectStoryboard(activate);
 1196  		comp->selectStoryboard("activate");
 1197  
 1198  		if (comp->isShowing())
 1199  			comp->startStoryboard();
 1200  	}
 1201  
 1202  	if (!recursive)
 1203  		return;
 1204  	
 1205  	for (auto child : comp->enumerateExtraChildrens())
 1206  		handleStoryBoard(child, activate, moveBy, false);
 1207  }
 1208  
 1209  bool DetailedContainer::hasActivationStoryboard(GuiComponent* comp, bool checkActivate, bool checkDeactivate)
 1210  {
 1211  	if (checkDeactivate && (comp->storyBoardExists("deactivate") || comp->storyBoardExists("deactivateNext") || comp->storyBoardExists("deactivatePrev")))	
 1212  		return true;
 1213  
 1214  	if (checkActivate && (comp->storyBoardExists("activate") || comp->storyBoardExists("activateNext") || comp->storyBoardExists("activatePrev")))
 1215  		return true;
 1216  
 1217  	return false;
 1218  }
 1219  
 1220  bool DetailedContainer::anyComponentHasStoryBoardRunning()
 1221  {
 1222  
 1223  	for (auto comp : getComponents())
 1224  		if (comp->isAnimationPlaying(0) || 
 1225  			comp->isStoryBoardRunning("activate") || 
 1226  			comp->isStoryBoardRunning("activateNext") || 
 1227  			comp->isStoryBoardRunning("activatePrev") || 
 1228  			comp->isStoryBoardRunning("deactivate") ||
 1229  			comp->isStoryBoardRunning("deactivateNext") ||
 1230  			comp->isStoryBoardRunning("deactivatePrev"))
 1231  			return true;
 1232  
 1233  	return false;
 1234  }
 1235  
 1236  bool DetailedContainer::anyComponentHasStoryBoard()
 1237  {
 1238  	for (auto comp : getComponents())
 1239  		if (hasActivationStoryboard(comp))
 1240  			return true;
 1241  
 1242  	return false;
 1243  }
 1244  
 1245  
 1246  void DetailedContainer::disableComponent(GuiComponent* comp)
 1247  {
 1248  	if (comp == nullptr)
 1249  		return;
 1250  
 1251  	if (mVideo == comp) { mVideo->setImage(""); mVideo->setVideo(""); }
 1252  	if (mImage == comp) mImage->setImage("");
 1253  	if (mThumbnail == comp) mThumbnail->setImage("");
 1254  	if (mFlag == comp) mFlag->setImage("");
 1255  	if (mManual == comp) mManual->setVisible(false);
 1256  	if (mNoManual == comp) mNoManual->setVisible(false);
 1257  	if (mMap == comp) mMap->setVisible(false);
 1258  	if (mNoMap == comp) mNoMap->setVisible(false);
 1259  	if (mSaveState == comp) mSaveState->setVisible(false);
 1260  	if (mNoSaveState == comp) mNoSaveState->setVisible(false);
 1261  	if (mKidGame == comp) mKidGame->setVisible(false);
 1262  	if (mNotKidGame == comp) mNotKidGame->setVisible(false);
 1263  	if (mGunGame == comp) mGunGame->setVisible(false);
 1264  	if (mNotGunGame == comp) mNotGunGame->setVisible(false);
 1265  	if (mWheelGame == comp) mWheelGame->setVisible(false);
 1266  	if (mNotWheelGame == comp) mNotWheelGame->setVisible(false);
 1267  	if (mTrackballGame == comp) mTrackballGame->setVisible(false);
 1268  	if (mNotTrackballGame == comp) mNotTrackballGame->setVisible(false);
 1269  	if (mSpinnerGame == comp) mSpinnerGame->setVisible(false);
 1270  	if (mNotSpinnerGame == comp) mNotSpinnerGame->setVisible(false);
 1271  	if (mCheevos == comp) mCheevos->setVisible(false);
 1272  	if (mNotCheevos == comp) mNotCheevos->setVisible(false);
 1273  	if (mNetplay == comp) mNetplay->setVisible(false);
 1274  	if (mNotNetplay == comp) mNotNetplay->setVisible(false);	
 1275  	if (mFavorite == comp) mFavorite->setVisible(false);
 1276  	if (mNotFavorite == comp) mNotFavorite->setVisible(false);
 1277  	if (mHidden == comp) mHidden->setVisible(false);
 1278  	
 1279  	for (auto& md : mdImages)
 1280  		if (md.component == comp)
 1281  			md.component->setImage("");
 1282  }
 1283  
 1284  std::vector<GuiComponent*>  DetailedContainer::getComponents()
 1285  {
 1286  	std::vector<GuiComponent*> comps;
 1287  
 1288  	for (auto lbl : getMetaComponents())
 1289  		if (lbl.component != nullptr)
 1290  			comps.push_back(lbl.component);
 1291  
 1292  	if (mVideo != nullptr) comps.push_back(mVideo);
 1293  	if (mImage != nullptr) comps.push_back(mImage);
 1294  	if (mThumbnail != nullptr) comps.push_back(mThumbnail);
 1295  	if (mFlag != nullptr) comps.push_back(mFlag);
 1296  	if (mManual != nullptr) comps.push_back(mManual);
 1297  	if (mNoManual != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNoManual);
 1298  	if (mMap != nullptr) comps.push_back(mMap);
 1299  	if (mNoMap != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNoMap);
 1300  	if (mSaveState != nullptr) comps.push_back(mSaveState);
 1301  	if (mNoSaveState != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNoSaveState);
 1302  	if (mKidGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mKidGame);
 1303  	if (mNotKidGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNotKidGame);
 1304  	if (mGunGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mGunGame);
 1305  	if (mNotGunGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNotGunGame);
 1306  	if (mWheelGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mWheelGame);
 1307  	if (mNotWheelGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNotWheelGame);
 1308  	if (mTrackballGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mTrackballGame);
 1309  	if (mNotTrackballGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNotTrackballGame);
 1310  	if (mSpinnerGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mSpinnerGame);
 1311  	if (mNotSpinnerGame != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNotSpinnerGame);
 1312  	if (mCheevos != nullptr) comps.push_back(mCheevos);
 1313  	if (mNotCheevos != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNotCheevos);
 1314  	if (mNetplay != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNetplay);
 1315  	if (mNotNetplay != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNotNetplay);	
 1316  	if (mFavorite != nullptr) comps.push_back(mFavorite);
 1317  	if (mNotFavorite != nullptr) comps.push_back(mNotFavorite);
 1318  	if (mHidden != nullptr) comps.push_back(mHidden);
 1319  
 1320  	for (auto& md : mdImages)
 1321  		if (md.component != nullptr)
 1322  			comps.push_back(md.component);
 1323  
 1324  	comps.push_back(&mDescription);
 1325  	comps.push_back(&mName);
 1326  	comps.push_back(&mRating);
 1327  
 1328  	for (auto lbl : getMetaComponents())
 1329  		if (lbl.label != nullptr)
 1330  			comps.push_back(lbl.label);
 1331  
 1332  	for (auto extra : mThemeExtras)
 1333  		comps.push_back(extra);
 1334  
 1335  	return comps;
 1336  }

 1340  	mParent = parent;

 1349  DetailedContainerHost::~DetailedContainerHost()
 1350  {

 1352

 1358  void DetailedContainerHost::onThemeChanged(const std::shared_ptr<ThemeData>& theme)
 1359  {
 1360  	mTheme = theme;
 1361  
 1362  	mContainer->onThemeChanged(theme);

 1366  
 1367  void DetailedContainerHost::update(int deltaTime)
 1368  {
 1369  	mContainer->updateFolderViewAmbiantProperties();
 1370  
 1371  	int index = mContainers.size();
 1372  	for (auto it = mContainers.begin(); it != mContainers.end(); )
 1373  	{
 1374  		DetailedContainer* dc = *it;
 1375  
 1376  		dc->updateFolderViewAmbiantProperties();
 1377  
 1378  		if (!dc->anyComponentHasStoryBoardRunning() || index > 4)
 1379  		{
 1380  			it = mContainers.erase(it);
 1381  
 1382  			for (auto cp : dc->getComponents())
 1383  			{
 1384  				cp->setVisible(false);
 1385  				cp->onHide();
 1386  			}
 1387  
 1388  			mWindow->postToUiThread([dc]() { delete dc; }, this);
 1389  			// break;
 1390  		}
 1391  		else
 1392  			++it;
 1393  
 1394  		index--;
 1395  	}
 1396  }
 1397  
 1398  Vector3f DetailedContainerHost::getLaunchTarget()
 1399  {
 1400  	return mContainer->getLaunchTarget();
 1401  }
 1402  
 1403  void DetailedContainerHost::updateControls(FileData* file, bool isClearing, int moveBy)
 1404  {
 1405  	if (!mContainer->anyComponentHasStoryBoard() || file == nullptr || isClearing || moveBy == 0)
 1406  	{
 1407  		if (file != nullptr && !isClearing)
 1408  			file->setSelectedGame();
 1409  
 1410  		mContainer->updateControls(file, isClearing, moveBy);
 1411  		return;
 1412  	}
 1413  	
 1414  	if (mActiveFile == file)
 1415  		return;
 1416  
 1417  	if (file == nullptr || isClearing)
 1418  	{
 1419  		mContainer->updateControls(nullptr, isClearing, moveBy, true);
 1420  		return;
 1421  	}
 1422  
 1423  	mActiveFile = file;
 1424  	mActiveFile->setSelectedGame();
 1425  	bool clear = isClearing;
 1426  	int by = moveBy;
 1427  
 1428  	mWindow->postToUiThread([this, clear, by]() 
 1429  	{ 		
 1430  		auto oldContainer = mContainer;
 1431  
 1432  		mContainers.push_back(mContainer);
 1433  		mContainer = new DetailedContainer(mParent, mList, mWindow, mViewType);
 1434  	
 1435  		if (mTheme != nullptr)
 1436  			mContainer->onThemeChanged(mTheme);
 1437  
 1438  		mContainer->updateControls(mActiveFile, clear, by);
 1439  		oldContainer->updateControls(nullptr, clear, by, true);
 1440  
 1441  		for (auto cp : mContainer->getComponents())
 1442  			cp->onShow();
 1443  	}, this);
 1444  }
 1445

 1448  	"This collection contains %i game, including: %s",
 1449  	"This collection contains %i games, including: %s", games_counter), games_counter, games_list.c_str());
 1450  */
```

```text
  131  const std::string GridGameListView::getImagePath(FileData* file)
  132  {
  133  	ImageSource src = mGrid.getImageSource();
  134  
  135  	if (src == ImageSource::IMAGE)
  136  		return file->getImagePath();
  137  
  138  	if (src == TITLESHOT && !file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::TitleShot).empty())
  139  		return file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::TitleShot);
  140  	else if (src == BOXART && !file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::BoxArt).empty())
  141  		return file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::BoxArt);
  142  	else if ((src == MARQUEE || src == ImageSource::MARQUEEORTEXT) && !file->getMarqueePath().empty())
  143  		return file->getMarqueePath();
  144  	else if ((src == IMAGE || src == TITLESHOT) && !file->getImagePath().empty())
  145  		return file->getImagePath();
  146  	else if (src == FANART && !file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::FanArt).empty())
  147  		return file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::FanArt);
  148  	else if (src == CARTRIDGE && !file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Cartridge).empty())
  149  		return file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Cartridge);
  150  	else if (src == MIX && !file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Mix).empty())
  151  		return file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Mix);
  152  
  153  	return file->getThumbnailPath();
  154  }
  155  
  156  void GridGameListView::populateList(const std::vector<FileData*>& files)
  157  {
  158  	updateHeaderLogoAndText();
  159  
  160  	mGrid.resetLastCursor();
  161  	mGrid.clear(); 
  162  	mGrid.resetLastCursor();
  163  
  164  	if (files.size() > 0)
  165  	{
  166  		bool showParentFolder = mRoot->getSystem()->getShowParentFolder();
  167  
  168  		if (mCursorStack.size() && showParentFolder)
  169  		{
  170  			auto top = mCursorStack.top();
  171  
  172  			std::string imagePath;
  173  		
  174  			// Find logo image from original system
  175  			if (mCursorStack.size() == 1 && top->getSystem()->isGroupChildSystem())
  176  			{
  177  				std::string startPath = top->getSystem()->getStartPath();
  178  				
  179  				auto parent = top->getSystem()->getParentGroupSystem();
  180  				
  181  				auto theme = parent->getTheme();
  182  				if (theme)
  183  				{
  184  					const ThemeData::ThemeElement* logoElem = theme->getElement("system", "logo", "image");
  185  					if (logoElem && logoElem->has("path"))
  186  						imagePath = logoElem->get<std::string>("path");
  187  				}
  188  
  189  				if (imagePath.empty())
  190  				{
  191  					for (auto child : parent->getRootFolder()->getChildren())
  192  					{
  193  						if (child->getPath() == startPath)
  194  						{
  195  							imagePath = child->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Image);
  196  							break;
  197  						}
  198  					}
  199  				}				
  200  			}
  201  			
  202  			mGrid.add(". .", imagePath, createParentFolderData());
  203  		}
  204  
  205  		GameNameFormatter formatter(mRoot->getSystem());
  206  
  207  		for (auto file : files)
  208  			mGrid.add(formatter.getDisplayName(file, file->getType() == FOLDER && Utils::FileSystem::exists(getImagePath(file))), getImagePath(file), file);
  209  
  210  		// if we have the ".." PLACEHOLDER, then select the first game instead of the placeholder
  211  		if (showParentFolder && mCursorStack.size() && mGrid.size() > 1 && mGrid.getCursorIndex() == 0)
  212  			mGrid.setCursorIndex(1);
  213  	}
  214  	else
  215  		addPlaceholder();
  216  
  217  	updateFolderPath();
  218  
  219  	mGrid.update(0);
  220  
  221  	if (mShowing)
  222  		onShow();
  223  }
  224  
  225  void GridGameListView::onThemeChanged(const std::shared_ptr<ThemeData>& theme)

  230  	mDetails.onThemeChanged(theme);
  231  	updateInfoPanel();

  234  void GridGameListView::updateInfoPanel()
  235  {
  236  	if (!mShowing)
  237  		return;
  238  
  239  	if (mRoot->getSystem()->isCollection())
  240  		updateHelpPrompts();
  241  
  242  	updateThemeExtrasBindings();
  243  
  244  	FileData* file = (mGrid.size() == 0 || mGrid.isScrolling()) ? NULL : mGrid.getSelected();
  245  	bool isClearing = mGrid.getObjects().size() == 0 && mGrid.getCursorIndex() == 0 && mGrid.getScrollingVelocity() == 0;
  246  	mDetails.updateControls(file, isClearing, mGrid.getCursorIndex() - mGrid.getLastCursor());
  247  }

  250  {
  251  	// empty grid - add a placeholder

  254  }
  255  
  256  void GridGameListView::launch(FileData* game)
  257  {
  258  	Vector3f target = mDetails.getLaunchTarget();
  259  	if (target == Vector3f(Renderer::getScreenWidth() / 2.0f, Renderer::getScreenHeight() / 2.0f, 0))
  260  	{
  261  		auto tile = mGrid.getSelectedTile();
  262  		if (tile != nullptr)
  263  			target = mGrid.getPosition() + tile->getLaunchTarget() - mGrid.getCameraOffset();
  264  	}
  265  
  266  	ViewController::get()->launch(game, target);
  267  }

  269  void GridGameListView::remove(FileData *game)
  270  {
  271  	mGrid.remove(game);
  272  
  273  	mRoot->removeFromVirtualFolders(game);
  274  	delete game;                                 // remove before repopulating (removes from parent)
  275  
  276  	if (mGrid.size() == 0)
  277  		addPlaceholder();
  278  
  279  	ViewController::get()->reloadGameListView(this);
  280  }

  315  void GridGameListView::moveToRandomGame()
  316  {
  317  	auto list = getFileDataEntries();
  318  
  319  	unsigned int total = (int)list.size();
  320  	if (total == 0)
  321  		return;
  322  
  323  	int target = Randomizer::random(total);
  324  	if (target >= 0 && target < total)
  325  	{
  326  		resetLastCursor();
  327  		setCursor(list.at(target));
  328  		if (isShowing())
  329  			onShow();
  330  	}
  331  }
```

```text
   32  ISimpleGameListView::ISimpleGameListView(Window* window, FolderData* root, bool temporary) : IGameListView(window, root),
   33  	mHeaderText(window), mHeaderImage(window), mBackground(window), mFolderPath(window), mOnExitPopup(nullptr), mLastParentFolderData(nullptr),
   34  	mYButton("y"), mXButton("x"), mOKButton("OK"), mSelectButton("select")
   35  {

   37  
   38  	mHeaderText.setText("Logo Text");

   40  	mHeaderText.setPosition(0, 0);
   41  	mHeaderText.setHorizontalAlignment(ALIGN_CENTER);
   42  	mHeaderText.setDefaultZIndex(50);
   43  	
   44  	mHeaderImage.setResize(0, mSize.y() * 0.185f);
   45  	mHeaderImage.setOrigin(0.5f, 0.0f);
   46  	mHeaderImage.setPosition(mSize.x() / 2, 0);
   47  	mHeaderImage.setDefaultZIndex(50);

   59

   63  ISimpleGameListView::~ISimpleGameListView()

   73  
   74  void ISimpleGameListView::onThemeChanged(const std::shared_ptr<ThemeData>& theme)
   75  {
   76  	using namespace ThemeFlags;
   77  	mBackground.applyTheme(theme, getName(), "background", ALL);
   78  	mHeaderImage.applyTheme(theme, getName(), "logo", ALL);
   79  	mHeaderText.applyTheme(theme, getName(), "logoText", ALL);
   80  	mFolderPath.applyTheme(theme, getName(), "folderpath", ALL);
   81  
   82  	// Remove old theme extras
   83  	for (auto extra : mThemeExtras)
   84  	{
   85  		removeChild(extra);
   86  		delete extra;
   87  	}
   88  	mThemeExtras.clear();
   89  
   90  	// Add new theme extras
   91  	mThemeExtras = ThemeData::makeExtras(theme, getName(), mWindow, false, mExtraMode);
   92  
   93  	for (auto extra : mThemeExtras)
   94  		addChild(extra);
   95  
   96  	if(mHeaderImage.hasImage())
   97  	{
   98  		removeChild(&mHeaderText);
   99  		addChild(&mHeaderImage);
  100  	}
  101  	else
  102  	{
  103  		addChild(&mHeaderText);
  104  		removeChild(&mHeaderImage);
  105  	}
  106  }
  107  
  108  void ISimpleGameListView::onFileChanged(FileData* /*file*/, FileChangeType /*change*/)
  109  {
  110  	// we could be tricky here to be efficient;
  111  	// but this shouldn't happen very often so we'll just always repopulate
  112  	FileData* cursor = getCursor();
  113  	if (!cursor->isPlaceHolder()) 
  114  	{
  115  		populateList(cursor->getParent()->getChildrenListToDisplay());
  116  		setCursor(cursor);
  117  	}
  118  	else
  119  	{
  120  		while (mCursorStack.size())
  121  			mCursorStack.pop();
  122  
  123  		populateList(mRoot->getChildrenListToDisplay());
  124  		setCursor(cursor);
  125  	}
  126  }

  128  void ISimpleGameListView::moveToFolder(FolderData* folder)

  131  		return;
  132  	
  133  	mCursorStack.push(folder);
  134  	populateList(folder->getChildrenListToDisplay());

  140  
  141  void ISimpleGameListView::toggleFavoritesFilter()
  142  {
  143  	FileData* cursor = getCursor();
  144  	if (cursor == nullptr)
  145  		return;
  146  
  147  	auto system = cursor->getSystem();
  148  
  149  	if (system->isGroupChildSystem())
  150  		system = system->getParentGroupSystem();
  151  
  152  	auto index = system->getIndex(true);
  153  	if (index == nullptr)
  154  		return;
  155  
  156  	static std::vector<std::string> trueFilter = { "TRUE" };
  157  
  158  	if (index->getFilter(FilterIndexType::FAVORITES_FILTER) == nullptr)
  159  		index->setFilter(FilterIndexType::FAVORITES_FILTER, &trueFilter);
  160  	else
  161  		index->setFilter(FilterIndexType::FAVORITES_FILTER, nullptr);
  162  
  163  	ViewController::get()->reloadGameListView(system);
  164  }
  165  
  166  FolderData* ISimpleGameListView::getCurrentFolder()
  167  {
  168  	if (mCursorStack.size())
  169  	{
  170  		auto top = mCursorStack.top();
  171  		if (top->getType() == FOLDER)
  172  			return (FolderData*)top;
  173  	}
  174  
  175  	return nullptr;
  176  }

  178  void ISimpleGameListView::update(const int deltaTime)
  179  {
  180  	GuiComponent::update(deltaTime);
  181  
  182  	if (mOKButton.isLongPressed(deltaTime))
  183  	{
  184  		if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("GameOptionsAtNorth"))
  185  			showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots();
  186  		else
  187  			showSelectedGameOptions();
  188  
  189  		return;
  190  	}
  191  
  192  	if (mSelectButton.isLongPressed(deltaTime))
  193  	{
  194  		toggleFavoritesFilter();
  195  		return;
  196  	}
  197  
  198  	if (mYButton.isLongPressed(deltaTime))
  199  	{
  200  		moveToRandomGame();
  201  		return;
  202  	}
  203  	
  204  	if (mXButton.isLongPressed(deltaTime))
  205  	{
  206  		if (UIModeController::getInstance()->isUIModeKid() && cursorHasSaveStatesEnabled())
  207  		{
  208  			if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("GameOptionsAtNorth"))
  209  				showSelectedGameOptions();
  210  			else
  211  				showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots();
  212  		}
  213  		else if (!UIModeController::getInstance()->isUIModeKid() && (mRoot->getSystem()->isGameSystem() || mRoot->getSystem()->isGroupSystem()))
  214  			CollectionSystemManager::get()->toggleGameInCollection(getCursor(), "Favorites");
  215  	}
  216  }

  218  void ISimpleGameListView::goBack()
  219  {
  220  	if (mCursorStack.size())
  221  	{
  222  		auto top = mCursorStack.top();
  223  		mCursorStack.pop();
  224  
  225  		FolderData* folder = top->getParent();
  226  		if (folder == nullptr && getCursor()->getSystem()->getParentGroupSystem() != nullptr)
  227  			folder = getCursor()->getSystem()->getParentGroupSystem()->getRootFolder();
  228  
  229  		if (folder != nullptr)
  230  		{
  231  			populateList(folder->getChildrenListToDisplay());
  232  			setCursor(top);
  233  			Sound::getFromTheme(getTheme(), getName(), "back")->play();
  234  		}
  235  	}
  236  	else if (mPopupSelfReference)
  237  	{
  238  		ViewController::get()->setActiveView(mPopupParentView);
  239  		mPopupParentView->updateHelpPrompts();
  240  		closePopupContext();	
  241  	}
  242  	else
  243  	{
  244  		onFocusLost();
  245  		SystemData* systemToView = getCursor()->getSystem();
  246  
  247  		if (systemToView->isGroupChildSystem())
  248  			systemToView = systemToView->getParentGroupSystem();
  249  		else if (systemToView->isCollection())
  250  			systemToView = CollectionSystemManager::get()->getSystemToView(systemToView);
  251  
  252  		ViewController::get()->goToSystemView(systemToView);
  253  	}	
  254  }
  255  
  256  bool ISimpleGameListView::input(InputConfig* config, Input input)
  257  {
  258  	if (mOKButton.isShortPressed(config, input))
  259  	{
  260  		launchSelectedGame();
  261  		return true;
  262  	}
  263  
  264  	if (mYButton.isShortPressed(config, input))
  265  	{
  266  		showQuickSearch();
  267  		return true;
  268  	}
  269  
  270  	if (mXButton.isShortPressed(config, input))
  271  	{
  272  		if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("GameOptionsAtNorth"))
  273  			showSelectedGameOptions();
  274  		else
  275  			showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots();
  276  
  277  		return true;
  278  	}	
  279  
  280  	if (!UIModeController::getInstance()->isUIModeKid() && mSelectButton.isShortPressed(config, input))
  281  	{
  282  		showGamelistOptions();
  283  		return true;
  284  	}
  285  
  286  	if (config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_OK, input) || config->isMappedTo("x", input) || config->isMappedTo("y", input) || config->isMappedTo("select", input))
  287  		return true;
  288  
  289  	if (input.value == 0)
  290  		return IGameListView::input(config, input);
  291  
  292  	if (config->isMappedTo("l3", input))
  293  	{
  294  		showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots();
  295  		return true;
  296  	}
  297  		
  298  	if (config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_BACK, input))
  299  	{
  300  		goBack();
  301  		return true;
  302  	}
  303  	else if ((Settings::getInstance()->getBool("QuickSystemSelect") && config->isMappedLike(getQuickSystemSelectRightButton(), input)) || (!Settings::getInstance()->getBool("QuickJumpLetter") && config->isMappedLike("righttrigger", input)))
  304  	{
  305  		if (!mPopupSelfReference)
  306  		{
  307  			onFocusLost();
  308  			ViewController::get()->goToNextGameList();
  309  		}
  310  
  311  		return true;
  312  	}
  313  	else if ((Settings::getInstance()->getBool("QuickSystemSelect") && config->isMappedLike(getQuickSystemSelectLeftButton(), input)) || (!Settings::getInstance()->getBool("QuickJumpLetter") && config->isMappedLike("lefttrigger", input)))
  314  	{
  315  		if (!mPopupSelfReference)
  316  		{
  317  			onFocusLost();
  318  			ViewController::get()->goToPrevGameList();
  319  		}
  320  
  321  		return true;
  322  	}
  323  	else if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("QuickJumpLetter") && config->isMappedLike("l2", input))
  324  	{
  325  		moveToPreviousLetter();
  326  		return true;
  327  	}
  328  	else if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("QuickJumpLetter") && config->isMappedLike("r2", input))
  329  	{
  330  		moveToNextLetter();
  331  		return true;
  332  	}
  333  	return IGameListView::input(config, input);
  334  }

  370  void ISimpleGameListView::showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots()
  371  {

  374  		return;

  376  	if (SaveStateRepository::isEnabled(cursor))

  391  
  392  void ISimpleGameListView::launchSelectedGame()
  393  {
  394  	// Don't launch game if transition is still running
  395  	if (ViewController::get()->isAnimationPlaying(0))
  396  		return;
  397  
  398  	FileData* cursor = getCursor();
  399  	FolderData* folder = NULL;
  400  
  401  	if (mCursorStack.size() && (cursor->getType() == PLACEHOLDER || cursor->getType() == FOLDER) && cursor->getPath() == "..")
  402  	{
  403  		auto top = mCursorStack.top();
  404  		mCursorStack.pop();
  405  
  406  		FolderData* folder = top->getParent();
  407  		if (folder == nullptr)
  408  			folder = getCursor()->getSystem()->getParentGroupSystem()->getRootFolder();
  409  
  410  		populateList(folder->getChildrenListToDisplay());
  411  		setCursor(top);
  412  		Sound::getFromTheme(getTheme(), getName(), "back")->play();
  413  	}
  414  	else
  415  	{
  416  		if (cursor->getType() == GAME)
  417  		{
  418  			if (SaveStateRepository::isEnabled(cursor) &&
  419  				(cursor->getCurrentGameSetting("savestates") == "1" || (cursor->getCurrentGameSetting("savestates") == "2" && cursor->getSourceFileData()->getSystem()->getSaveStateRepository()->hasSaveStates(cursor))))
  420  			{
  421  				mWindow->pushGui(new GuiSaveState(mWindow, cursor, [this, cursor](SaveState* state)
  422  				{
  423  					Sound::getFromTheme(getTheme(), getName(), "launch")->play();
  424  
  425  					LaunchGameOptions options;
  426  					options.saveStateInfo = state;
  427  					ViewController::get()->launch(cursor, options);
  428  				}
  429  				));
  430  			}
  431  			else
  432  			{
  433  				Sound::getFromTheme(getTheme(), getName(), "launch")->play();
  434  				launch(cursor);
  435  			}
  436  		}
  437  		else if (cursor->getType() == FOLDER)
  438  			moveToFolder((FolderData*)cursor);
  439  	}
  440  }
```
