```text
  443  void ISimpleGameListView::showQuickSearch()
  444  {
  445  	std::string searchText;
  446  
  447  	auto idx = mRoot->getSystem()->getIndex(false);
  448  	if (idx != nullptr)
  449  		searchText = idx->getTextFilter();
  450  
  451  	auto updateVal = [this](const std::string& newVal)
  452  	{
  453  		auto index = mRoot->getSystem()->getIndex(!newVal.empty());
  454  		if (index != nullptr)
  455  		{
  456  			index->setTextFilter(newVal);
  457  			if (!index->isFiltered())
  458  				mRoot->getSystem()->deleteIndex();
  459  		}
  460  
  461  		if (mRoot->getSystem()->isCollection())
  462  			CollectionSystemManager::get()->reloadCollection(mRoot->getSystem()->getName());
  463  		else
  464  			ViewController::get()->reloadGameListView(mRoot->getSystem());
  465  	};
  466  
  467  	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("UseOSK"))
  468  		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopupKeyboard(mWindow, _("FILTER GAMES BY TEXT"), searchText, updateVal, false));
  469  	else
  470  		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopup(mWindow, _("FILTER GAMES BY TEXT"), searchText, updateVal, false));
  471  }
  472  
  473  void ISimpleGameListView::moveToRandomGame()
  474  {
  475  	auto list = getFileDataEntries();
  476  
  477  	unsigned int total = (int)list.size();
  478  	if (total == 0)
  479  		return;
  480  
  481  	int target = Randomizer::random(total); // (int)Math::round((std::rand() / (float)RAND_MAX) * (total - 1));
  482  	if (target >= 0 && target < total)
  483  		setCursor(list.at(target));
  484  }
  485  
  486  bool ISimpleGameListView::moveToLetter(const std::string& letter)
  487  {
  488  	auto files = getFileDataEntries();
  489  	long letterIndex = -1;
  490  
  491  	for (int i = files.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
  492  	{
  493  		const std::string& name = files.at(i)->getName();
  494  		if (name.empty())
  495  			continue;
  496  
  497  		std::string checkLetter;
  498  
  499  		if (Utils::String::isKorean(name.c_str()))
  500  		{
  501  			const char* koreanLetter = nullptr;
  502  
  503  			std::string nameChar = name.substr(0, 3);
  504  			if (!Utils::String::splitHangulSyllable(nameChar.c_str(), &koreanLetter) || !koreanLetter)
  505  				continue; // Korean supports chosung search only.
  506  			else
  507  				checkLetter = std::string(koreanLetter, 3);
  508  		}
  509  		else
  510  		{
  511  			checkLetter = std::string(1, toupper(name[0]));
  512  		}
  513  
  514  		if (letterIndex >= 0 && checkLetter != letter)
  515  			break;
  516  
  517  		if (checkLetter == letter)
  518  			letterIndex = i;
  519  	}
  520  
  521  	if (letterIndex >= 0) {
  522  		setCursor(files.at(letterIndex));
  523  		return true;
  524  	}
  525  
  526  	return false;
  527  }
  528  
  529  void ISimpleGameListView::moveToLetterByOffset(int offset)
  530  {
  531  	std::vector<std::string> letters = getEntriesLetters();
  532  	if (letters.empty())
  533  		return;
  534  
  535  	FileData* game = getCursor();
  536  	if (game == nullptr)
  537  		return;
  538  
  539  	const std::string& namecurrent = game->getName();
  540  	std::string curLetter;
  541  
  542  	if (Utils::String::isKorean(namecurrent.c_str()))
  543  	{
  544  		const char* koreanLetter = nullptr;
  545  
  546  		std::string nameChar = namecurrent.substr(0, 3);
  547  		if (!Utils::String::splitHangulSyllable(nameChar.c_str(), &koreanLetter) || !koreanLetter)
  548  			curLetter = std::string(letters.at(0)); // Korean supports chosung search only. set default.
  549  		else
  550  			curLetter = std::string(koreanLetter, 3);
  551  	}
  552  	else
  553  	{
  554  		curLetter = std::string(1, toupper(namecurrent[0]));
  555  	}
  556  
  557  	auto it = std::find(letters.begin(), letters.end(), curLetter);
  558  	if (it != letters.end())
  559  	{
  560  		int index = std::distance(letters.begin(), it) + offset;
  561  		if (index < 0)
  562  			index = letters.size() - 1;
  563  		else if (index >= letters.size())
  564  			index = 0;
  565  
  566  		moveToLetter(letters.at(index));
  567  	}
  568  }
  569  
  570  void ISimpleGameListView::moveToNextLetter()
  571  {
  572  	moveToLetterByOffset(1);
  573  }
  574  
  575  void ISimpleGameListView::moveToPreviousLetter()
  576  {
  577  	moveToLetterByOffset(-1);
  578  }
  579  
  580  std::vector<std::string> ISimpleGameListView::getEntriesLetters()
  581  {	
  582  	std::set<std::string> setOfLetters;
  583  
  584  	for (auto file : getFileDataEntries())
  585  	{
  586  		if (file->getType() != GAME)
  587  			continue;
  588  
  589  		const std::string& name = file->getName();
  590  
  591  		if (Utils::String::isKorean(name.c_str()))
  592  		{
  593  			const char* koreanLetter = nullptr;
  594  
  595  			std::string nameChar = name.substr(0, 3);
  596  			if (!Utils::String::splitHangulSyllable(nameChar.c_str(), &koreanLetter) || !koreanLetter)
  597  				continue;
  598  
  599  			setOfLetters.insert(std::string(koreanLetter, 3));
  600  		}
  601  		else
  602  		{
  603  			setOfLetters.insert(std::string(1, toupper(name[0])));
  604  		}
  605  	}
  606  
  607  	std::vector<std::string> letters(setOfLetters.begin(), setOfLetters.end());
  608  	return letters;
  609  }

  622

  624  }

  651  void ISimpleGameListView::setPopupContext(std::shared_ptr<IGameListView> pThis, std::shared_ptr<GuiComponent> parentView, const std::string label, const std::function<void()>& onExitTemporary)
  652  { 
  653  	mPopupSelfReference = pThis;
  654  	mPopupParentView = parentView;
  655  	mOnExitPopup = onExitTemporary;
  656  
  657  	if (mHeaderImage.hasImage())
  658  	{
  659  		mHeaderText.setText(_("Games similar to") + " " + label); // 
  660  
  661  		mHeaderImage.setImage("");
  662  		addChild(&mHeaderText);
  663  		removeChild(&mHeaderImage);
  664  	}
  665  }
  666  
  667  void ISimpleGameListView::closePopupContext()
  668  {
  669  	if (!mPopupSelfReference)
  670  		return;
  671  
  672  	auto exitPopup = mOnExitPopup;
  673  
  674  	mPopupParentView.reset();	
  675  	mPopupSelfReference.reset();
  676  
  677  	if (exitPopup != nullptr)
  678  		exitPopup();
  679  }
  680  
  681  std::vector<HelpPrompt> ISimpleGameListView::getHelpPrompts()
  682  {
  683  	std::vector<HelpPrompt> prompts;
  684  
  685  	if (Renderer::getScreenProportion() > 1.4)
  686  	{
  687  		if (mPopupSelfReference == nullptr && Settings::getInstance()->getBool("QuickSystemSelect") && getQuickSystemSelectLeftButton() == "left")
  688  			prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("left/right", _("SYSTEM")));
  689  
  690  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("up/down", _("CHOOSE")));
  691  	}
  692  
  693  	bool invertNorthButton = Settings::getInstance()->getBool("GameOptionsAtNorth");
  694  	const bool kidMode = UIModeController::getInstance()->isUIModeKid();
  695  
  696  	// What the buttons on this page actually do, so the bar promises nothing
  697  	// else (#210). X opens the game options with GAME OPTIONS AT NORTH on and
  698  	// the save state manager with it off (the short-press handler above), and
  699  	// the manager refuses a cursor that has no save states at all
  700  	// (showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots -> SaveStateRepository::isEnabled). So
  701  	// SAVE STATES is named only where it can happen: it used to be on the A
  702  	// prompt of every list -- SCREENSHOTS, TOOLS, an image viewer, a system
  703  	// with no save state config -- where the hold did nothing and the same
  704  	// row already said FAVORITE on X because the gate below had refused it.
  705  	// GAME OPTIONS is named wherever X opens it, which the FAVORITE-only
  706  	// fallback used to hide on exactly those lists.
  707  	const bool saveStates = cursorHasSaveStatesEnabled();
  708  
  709  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_BACK, _("BACK"), [&] { goBack(); }));
  710  
  711  	if (invertNorthButton)
  712  	{
  713  		if (saveStates)
  714  			prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_OK, _("SAVE STATES (HOLD)"), [&] { showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots(); }));
  715  	}
  716  	else 
  717  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_OK, _("GAME OPTIONS (HOLD)"), [&] { showSelectedGameOptions(); }));
  718  
  719  	if (!kidMode)
  720  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("select", _("OPTIONS"), [&] { showGamelistOptions(); }));
  721  
  722  	if (invertNorthButton)
  723  	{
  724  		if (kidMode)
  725  			prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("x", _("GAME OPTIONS"), [&] { showSelectedGameOptions(); }));
  726  		else
  727  			prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("x", _("GAME OPTIONS") + std::string("/") + _("FAVORITE"), [&] { showSelectedGameOptions(); }));
  728  	}
  729  	else if (saveStates)
  730  	{
  731  		if (kidMode)
  732  			prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("x", _("SAVE STATES"), [&] { showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots(); }));
  733  		else
  734  			prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("x", _("SAVE STATES") + std::string("/") + _("FAVORITE"), [&] { showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots(); }));
  735  	}
  736  	else if (!kidMode)
  737  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("x", _("FAVORITE")));
  738  
  739  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt("y", _("SEARCH") + std::string("/") + _("RANDOM"), [&] { showQuickSearch(); }));
  740  
  741  	return prompts;
  742  }
  743  
  744  void ISimpleGameListView::updateThemeExtrasBindings()
  745  {
  746  	FileData* file = getCursor();
  747  	if (file == nullptr)
  748  		return;
  749  
  750  	auto system = file->getSystem();
  751  
  752  	for (auto extra : mThemeExtras)
  753  		BindingManager::updateBindings(extra, file);
  754  
  755  	// A screenshot under the SCREENSHOTS entry is drawn at the aspect of the
  756  	// system it was taken in (fork #243, D-UI-080). The picture a theme binds
  757  	// with {game:image} is an extra of this view, so it is applied here;
  758  	// the details container does the same for its md_image and its own
  759  	// extras. A file of any other system resets them.
  760  	const bool screenshots = system != nullptr && system->hasPlatformId(PlatformIds::IMAGEVIEWER);
  761  	const DisplayAspect::Transform transform = screenshots ? DisplayAspect::forScreenshot(file) : DisplayAspect::Transform();
  762  	const std::string imageBound = file->getImagePath();
  763  	for (auto extra : mThemeExtras)
  764  		DisplayAspect::applyToBoundImages(extra, imageBound, transform);
  765  }
  766  
  767  bool ISimpleGameListView::onAction(const std::string& action)
  768  {
  769  	if (action == "back")
  770  	{
  771  		goBack();
  772  		return true;
  773  	}
  774  
  775  	if (action == "options")
  776  	{
  777  		showGamelistOptions();
  778  		return true;
  779  	}
  780  
  781  	if (action == "gameoptions")
  782  	{
  783  		showSelectedGameOptions();
  784  		return true;
  785  	}
  786  
  787  	if (action == "launch")
  788  	{
  789  		launchSelectedGame();
  790  		return true;
  791  	}
  792  
  793  	if (action == "search")
  794  	{
  795  		showQuickSearch();
  796  		return true;
  797  	}
  798  
  799  	if (action == "savestates")
  800  	{
  801  		showSelectedGameSaveSnapshots();
  802  		return true;
  803  	}
  804  
  805  	if (action == "favorite")
  806  	{
  807  		toggleFavoritesFilter();
  808  		return true;
  809  	}
  810  	
  811  	if (action == "random")
  812  	{
  813  		moveToRandomGame();
  814  		return true;
  815  	}
  816  	
  817  	if (action == "video")
  818  	{
  819  		FileData* game = getCursor();
  820  		if (game != nullptr)
  821  		{
  822  			auto path = game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Video);
  823  			if (!path.empty())
  824  				GuiVideoViewer::playVideo(mWindow, path);
  825  		}
  826  		return true;
  827  	}
  828  
  829  	if (action == "manual")
  830  	{
  831  		FileData* game = getCursor();
  832  		if (game != nullptr)
  833  		{
  834  			auto path = game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Manual);
  835  			if (!path.empty())
  836  				GuiImageViewer::showPdf(mWindow, path);
  837  		}
  838  		return true;
  839  	}
  840  
  841  	if (action == "map")
  842  	{
  843  		FileData* game = getCursor();
  844  		if (game != nullptr)
  845  		{
  846  			auto path = game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Map);
  847  			if (!path.empty())
  848  				GuiImageViewer::showImage(mWindow, path, Utils::String::toLower(Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(path)) != ".pdf");
  849  		}
  850  		return true;
  851  	}
  852  
  853  	if (action == "medias")
  854  	{
  855  		FileData* game = getCursor();
  856  		if (game != nullptr)
  857  		{
  858  			auto imageList = game->getSourceFileData()->getFileMedias();
  859  			if (imageList.size())
  860  				GuiImageViewer::showImages(mWindow, imageList);
  861  		}
  862  
  863  		return true;
  864  	}
  865  
  866  	if (action == "cheevos")
  867  	{
  868  		FileData* game = getCursor();
  869  		if (game != nullptr)
  870  		{
  871  			auto path = Utils::String::toInteger(game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosId));
  872  			if (path != 0)
  873  				GuiGameAchievements::show(mWindow, path, game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosHash));
  874  		}
  875  
  876  		return true;
  877  	}
  878  
  879  	
  880  	return false;
  881  }
  882  
  883  FolderData* ISimpleGameListView::createParentFolderData()
  884  {
  885  	SystemData* system = mCursorStack.size() ? mCursorStack.top()->getSystem() : mRoot->getSystem();
  886  	auto folder = new FolderData("..", system);
  887  	folder->setMetadata(MetaDataId::Name, ". .");
  888  
  889  	std::string dest = mRoot->getSystem()->getName();
  890  
  891  	if (mCursorStack.size() > 1)
  892  	{
  893  		std::stack<FileData*> tempStack = mCursorStack;
  894  		tempStack.pop();
  895  
  896  		FileData* top = tempStack.top();
  897  		
  898  		dest = top->getName();
  899  		folder->setMetadata(MetaDataId::Image, top->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Image));
  900  		folder->setMetadata(MetaDataId::Thumbnail, top->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Thumbnail));
  901  		folder->setMetadata(MetaDataId::Marquee, top->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Marquee));
  902  	}
  903  
  904  	auto backTo = Utils::String::format(_("Back to %s").c_str(), dest.c_str());
  905  	folder->setMetadata(MetaDataId::Desc, backTo); // _("Parent folder")
  906  
  907  	if (mCursorStack.size() <= 1)
  908  	{
  909  		auto logo = mRoot->getSystem()->getProperty("logo");
  910  		if (Utils::FileSystem::exists(logo.toString()))
  911  		{
  912  			folder->setMetadata(MetaDataId::Image, logo.toString());
  913  			folder->setMetadata(MetaDataId::Marquee, logo.toString());
  914  		}
  915  	}
  916  
  917  	if (mLastParentFolderData != nullptr)
  918  		delete mLastParentFolderData;
  919  
  920  	mLastParentFolderData = folder;
  921  
  922  	return folder;
  923  }
  924  
  925  
  926  FileData* ISimpleGameListView::createNoEntriesPlaceholder()
  927  {
  928  	// empty grid - add a placeholder
  929  	FileData* placeholder = new FileData(PLACEHOLDER, "<" + _("No Entries Found") + ">", mRoot->getSystem());
  930  
  931  	if (mLastParentFolderData != nullptr)
  932  		delete mLastParentFolderData;
  933  
  934  	mLastParentFolderData = placeholder;
  935  
  936  	return placeholder;
  937  }
```

```text
  593  		LOG(LogWarning) << warn;
  594  		return true;
  595  	}
  596  	const Value& games = doc["data"]["games"];
  597  
  598  	if (!doc.HasMember("include") || !doc["include"].HasMember("boxart"))
  599  	{
  600  		std::string warn = "TheGamesDBJSONRequest - Response had no include boxart data.\n";
  601  		LOG(LogWarning) << warn;
  602  		return true;
  603  	}
  604  
  605  	const Value& boxart = doc["include"]["boxart"];
  606  
  607  	if (!boxart.HasMember("base_url") || !boxart.HasMember("data") || !boxart.IsObject())
  608  	{
  609  		std::string warn = "TheGamesDBJSONRequest - Response include had no usable boxart data.\n";
  610  		LOG(LogWarning) << warn;
  611  		return true;
  612  	}
  613  
  614  	resources.ensureResources();
```

```text
  162  	}
  163
```

```text
  324  		return false;
  325  	}
  326  
  327  	if (!doc.IsArray() || !doc.Size())
  328  	{
  329  		std::string warn = "IGDBRequest - Response is not valid.\n";
  330  		LOG(LogWarning) << warn;
  331  		return false;
  332  	}
  333  
  334  	for (int i = 0; i < (int)doc.Size(); ++i)
  335  	{
  336  		auto& v = doc[i];
  337  
  338  		try
  339  		{
  340  			processIgdbGame(v, results, mIsArcade);
  341  		}
  342  		catch (std::runtime_error& e)
  343  		{
  344  			LOG(LogError) << "Error while processing game: " << e.what();
  345  		}
```

```text
  114  std::string HttpServerThread::getMimeType(const std::string &path)
  115  {
  116  	auto ext = Utils::String::toLower(Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(path));
  117  	if (ext[0] == '.')
  118  		ext = ext.substr(1);
  119  
  120  	auto it = mimeTypes.find(ext);
  121  	if (it != mimeTypes.cend())
  122  		return it->second;
  123  	
  124  	return "text/plain";
  125  }
```

```text
  279  	if (value >= sz) value -= sz;
```

```text
  540  		return true;
  541  	}
  542  
  543  	return false;
```

```text
   54  
   55  bool UIModeController::listen(InputConfig * config, Input input)
   56  {
   57  	// Reads the current input to listen for the passkey
   58  	// sequence to unlock the UI mode. The progress is saved in mPassKeyCounter
   59  	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("Debug"))
   60  	{
   61  		logInput(config, input);
   62  	}
   63  
   64  	if ((Settings::getInstance()->getString("UIMode") == "Full") || !isValidInput(config, input))
   65  	{
   66  		return false; // Already unlocked, or invalid input, nothing to do here.
   67  	}
   68  
   69  
   70  	if (!inputIsMatch(config, input))
   71  	{
   72  		mPassKeyCounter = 0; // current input is incorrect, reset counter
   73  	}
   74  
   75  	if (mPassKeyCounter == (int)mPassKeySequence.length())
   76  	{
   77  		unlockUIMode();
   78  		return true;
   79  	}
   80  	return false;
   81  }
   82  
   83  bool UIModeController::inputIsMatch(InputConfig * config, Input input)
   84  {
   85  	for (auto valstring : mInputVals)
   86  	{
   87  		if (config->isMappedLike(valstring, input) &&
   88  			(mPassKeySequence[mPassKeyCounter] == valstring[0]))
   89  		{
   90  			mPassKeyCounter++;
   91  			return true;
   92  		}
   93  	}
   94  	return false;
   95  }
   96  
   97  // When we have reached the end of the list, trigger UI_mode unlock
   98  void UIModeController::unlockUIMode()
   99  {
  100  	LOG(LogDebug) << " UIModeController::listen(): Passkey sequence completed, switching UIMode to full";
  101  
  102  	Settings::getInstance()->setString("UIMode", "Full");
  103  	Settings::getInstance()->saveFile();
  104  	mPassKeyCounter = 0;
  105  }
```

```text
   14  // Handle for some asynchronous operation.

   16  {
   17  public:
   18  	AsyncHandle() : mStatus(ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS), mErrorCode(200) {};
   19  	virtual ~AsyncHandle() {};
   20

   30  	// User-friendly string of our current status.  Will return error message if status() == SEARCH_ERROR.
   31  	inline std::string getStatusString()
   32  	{

   34  		{
   35  		case ASYNC_IN_PROGRESS:
   36  			return "in progress";
   37  		case ASYNC_ERROR:
   38  			return mError;
   39  		case ASYNC_DONE:
   40  			return "done";
   41  		default:
   42  			return "something impossible has occured; row, row, fight the power";
   43  		}
   44  	}
   45  
   46  protected:

   48
```

```text
  194  
  195  /// This class is used to call Scraper->search() in a thread. 
  196  /// This avoids freezes while building requests, as they can have some blocking code ( build rom md5, ask server for a token... )
  197  class ScraperAsyncRequestsBuilder : public ScraperSearchHandle
  198  {
  199  public:
  200  	ScraperAsyncRequestsBuilder(Scraper* scraper, ScraperSearch* ss)
  201  	{
  202  		mScraper = scraper;		
  203  		mSearch = ss;
  204  
  205  		mFinished = false;
  206  		mThread = new std::thread(&ScraperAsyncRequestsBuilder::loadSearchHandleAsync, this);
  207  	}
  208  	
  209  	void loadSearchHandleAsync()
  210  	{
  211  		try
  212  		{
  213  			mSearchHandle = mScraper->search(mSearch->params);
  214  		}
  215  		catch (std::exception& e)
  216  		{ 
  217  			mError = e.what();
  218  		}
  219  
  220  		mFinished = true;
  221  	}
  222  
  223  	virtual void update()
  224  	{
  225  		if (!mFinished)
  226  			return;
  227  
  228  		mFinished = false;
  229  
  230  		if (mThread && mThread->joinable())
  231  		{
  232  			mThread->join();
  233  			mThread = nullptr;
  234  		}
  235  
  236  		if (mSearchHandle == nullptr)
  237  			setError(mError);
  238  		else
  239  			setStatus(ASYNC_DONE);
  240  	}
  241  
  242  	ScraperSearchHandle* ExtractSearchHandle()
  243  	{
  244  		if (mSearchHandle)
  245  			return mSearchHandle.release();
  246  
  247  		return nullptr;
  248  	}
  249  
  250  private:
  251  	Scraper*		mScraper;
  252  	ScraperSearch*	mSearch;
  253  	std::thread*	mThread;
  254  
  255  	std::string		mError;
  256  	bool			mFinished;
  257  
  258  	std::unique_ptr<ScraperSearchHandle> mSearchHandle;
  259  };
  260  
  261  void ScraperSearchComponent::search(const ScraperSearchParams& params)
  262  {
  263  	if (mSearchStartingCallback)
  264  		mSearchStartingCallback();
  265  
  266  	for (auto pair : mMD_Pairs)
  267  	{
  268  		pair.first->setVisible(false);
  269  		pair.second->setVisible(false);
  270  	}
  271  
  272  	mInfoPaneCursor = -1;
  273  	mInitialSearch = params;
  274  	mBlockAccept = true;
  275  
  276  	for (auto scrapper : mScrapEngines)
  277  		delete scrapper;
  278  
  279  	mScrapEngines.clear();
  280  
  281  	mResultList->clear();
  282  	mMDResolveHandle.reset();
  283  	updateInfoPane();
  284  
  285  	auto excludedScrapers = Utils::String::split(Settings::getInstance()->getString("DisabledManualScrapers"), ';');
  286  
  287  	for (auto scraperName : Scraper::getScraperList())
  288  	{
  289  		auto scraper = Scraper::getScraper(scraperName);
  290  		if (scraper == nullptr || !scraper->isSupportedPlatform(params.system))
  291  			continue;
  292  
  293  		if (std::find(excludedScrapers.cbegin(), excludedScrapers.cend(), scraperName) != excludedScrapers.cend())
  294  			continue;		
  295  
  296  		ScraperSearch* ss = new ScraperSearch();
  297  		ss->name = scraperName;
  298  		ss->params = params;
  299  		ss->params.isManualScrape = true;			
  300  		if (params.nameOverride.empty())
  301  			ss->searchHandle = std::make_unique<ScraperAsyncRequestsBuilder>(scraper, ss);
  302  		else
  303  			ss->searchHandle = scraper->search(ss->params);
  304  
  305  		mScrapEngines.push_back(ss);
  306  	}
  307  	
  308  	if (mScrapEngines.size() == 0)
  309  	{
  310  		std::string scraperName = Settings::getInstance()->getString("Scraper");
  311  		auto scraper = Scraper::getScraper(scraperName);
  312  
  313  		ScraperSearch* ss = new ScraperSearch();
  314  		ss->name = scraperName;
  315  		ss->params = params;
  316  		ss->params.isManualScrape = true;
  317  
  318  		if (params.nameOverride.empty() && scraper != nullptr && scraper->isSupportedPlatform(params.system))
  319  			ss->searchHandle = std::make_unique<ScraperAsyncRequestsBuilder>(scraper, ss);
```

```text
  104  
  105  // What cancelling means, read at the moment of deciding (D-UI-023): each
  106  // game's result is saved as it comes in, so what is scraped stays, and the
  107  // game lists show it once they are updated. YES first, NO last so B
  108  // answers NO. Nothing of the page is captured: the stop is a static call on
  109  // the run in flight, and the page shows the outcome as the run ends.
  110  void GuiScraperRun::askCancel()
  111  {
  112  	mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow,
  113  		_("CANCEL SCRAPING?") + std::string("\n\n")
  114  			+ _("WHAT'S SCRAPED SO FAR IS KEPT. UPDATE GAMELISTS TO APPLY IT."),
  115  		_("YES"), [] { ThreadedScraper::stop(); },
  116  		_("NO"), nullptr));
  117  }
  118  
  119  std::vector<HelpPrompt> GuiScraperRun::getHelpPrompts()
  120  {
  121  	std::vector<HelpPrompt> prompts;
  122  	const ThreadedScraper::Progress p = ThreadedScraper::progress();
  123  	if (!p.finished)
  124  	{
  125  		prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_BACK, _("CANCEL")));
  126  		return prompts;
  127  	}
  128  	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_BACK, _("CLOSE")));
  129  	return prompts;
  130  }
  131  
  132  // The word for the run (D-UI-028): COMPLETED when every game was looked
  133  // at; SKIPPED for the player's own cancel (nothing went wrong, and the
  134  // games not reached are the next scrape's); COULDN'T FINISH when the
  135  // scraper refused the run -- the why on line 4 is its own words -- or when
  136  // games among many could not be scraped (a run whose parts disagree is
  137  // COULDN'T FINISH, D-UI-030).
  138  std::string GuiScraperRun::outcomeWord(const ThreadedScraper::Progress& p)
  139  {
  140  	if (p.cancelled)
  141  		return _("SKIPPED - YOU CANCELLED IT");
  142  	if (p.failed || p.errors > 0)
  143  		return _("COULDN'T FINISH");
  144  	return _("COMPLETED");
  145  }
  146  
  147  void GuiScraperRun::render(const Transform4x4f& parentTrans)
  148  {
  149  	Transform4x4f trans = parentTrans * getTransform();
  150  	Renderer::setMatrix(trans);
  151  	// Dim the whole screen: this is a takeover, and the carousel showing
  152  	// through would say the app is idle while a scrape is in flight.
  153  	Renderer::drawRect(0.f, 0.f, mSize.x(), mSize.y(), 0x000000D0);
  154  
  155  	mBackground.render(trans);
  156  
  157  	for (auto& t : { mTitle, mStatus, mCounter, mActivity, mDetail, mNote, mElapsed, mFooter })
  158  		t->render(trans);
```
